TL;DR: 本文研究LLM-as-a-judge在闭环再生（closed-loop regeneration）中的有效性，以表格识别为测试平台。研究发现：评判信号弱（分数频繁平局、排名不可复现）；无约束再生导致严重损失，而结构保持指令可降低严重损失率但未带来整体提升；验证了评估能力不等于优化效用，迭代精炼需要确定性的结构变化检测信号而非单纯的评判分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge被广泛用于闭环再生中提供反馈和选择信号，但其有效性尚未得到充分验证。作者利用表格识别任务中确定性TEDS指标作为受控测试平台，系统检验LLM评判在迭代优化中的实际效用。

Method: 以FinTabNet和OmniDocBench两个表格识别数据集为测试平台，将LLM-as-a-judge用于闭环再生流程。通过比较评判分数分布、排名可复现性、选择策略效果，以及引入结构保持指令（structure-preserving instruction）对比无约束再生，进行系统的受控实验分析，并开展探索性2×2分析。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1)LLM评判信号弱——分数频繁平局，排名不可复现，唯一优于随机的选择策略依赖最早迭代平局规则，无法归因于评判分数；(2)无约束再生即使无特定评判反馈也会产生严重损失，结构保持指令在FinTabNet上显著降低严重损失率，在OmniDocBench上方向一致；(3)结构保持约束减少了严重损失尾部但未带来整体性能提升。在2×2探索分析中，保留评判反馈时该保护效应不再稳定可观测。

Conclusion: 评估能力不等于优化效用。迭代精炼至少需要一个能确定性地检测结构变化的验证信号，而非仅依赖LLM评判分数。LLM作为评估者仍有价值，但不应将其评估能力等同于优化指导能力。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge is widely used to provide feedback and selection signals in closedloop regeneration, but this use remains insufficiently validated. We study it in table recognition, where deterministic TEDS evaluation provides a controlled testbed, using FinTabNet and OmniDocBench. Three findings emerge. First, judge signals were weak on both datasets: scores frequently tied, rankings were not reproducible, and the only selection policy that beat random on both datasets depended on an earliest-iteration tie rule, so its advantage cannot be attributed to the judge scores alone. Iteration produced better candidates, but the judge failed to recover them. Second, severe losses occurred even without specific judge feedback. A structurepreserving instruction significantly reduced the severe-loss rate on FinTabNet and was directionally consistent on OmniDocBench. The contrasts support target-preservation failure under unconstrained regeneration as a proximate mechanism of the observed severe losses. Third, the structure-preservation constraint reduced the severe-loss tail but produced no improvement. In an exploratory 2x2 analysis, the same protection was not stably observed when judge feedback was retained. These results do not dispute the value of LLMs as evaluators. Instead, they show that evaluation ability does not imply optimization utility. Iterative refinement requires, at minimum, a verification signal that deterministically detects structural change, rather than judge scores alone.

</details>


### [101] [A POS Tier Is the Key to Automated Annotation for Low-Resource Language Documentation: Neural Interlinear Glossing for Irabu, a Southern Ryukyuan Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13372)
*Michinori Shimoji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用小型BiLSTM-CRF模型，为濒危语言Irabu Ryukyuan构建了一个完整的形态素标注流水线（分词、词性标注、标注）。研究发现，在训练数据中增加词性标注层（POS tier）能显著提升语法标注（glossing）的准确性，尤其在数据稀缺时效果更明显。尽管当前自动词性标注器的误差（12%错误率）阻碍了全自动流水线中这一增益的完全实现，但通过噪声模拟实验证明，随着标注器准确率提升至92-96%，增益可被逐步回收。作者建议田野语言学工作者采用四行标注规范：文本、词性、注解、翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田野语言学中，语篇数据的逐行标注（interlinearization）极其耗时（每分钟录音需约一小时人工标注）。对于濒危语言，与母语者验证分析的时间本就有限，因此自动化部分标注流程具有直接的文献记录价值。本文旨在探究在极度稀缺的数据条件下（约一小时标注语料），标注的丰富程度（是否含词性标注层）和数量如何影响自动标注质量。

Method: 以Irabu Ryukyuan语为对象，构建基于小型透明BiLSTM-CRF模型的完整神经标注流水线（包括形态素切分、词性标注、语法注解）。在严格控制的数据预算下（训练数据从6分钟到47分钟不等），操控两个变量：标注的丰富度（有/无POS层）和标注数量。此外，通过对金标POS施加受控噪声，模拟不同准确率下POS对glossing的增益回收曲线。

Result: （1）金标POS将语法标注准确率提升+4.4点（SD 0.7），在数据量缩减至四分之一时增益扩大至+11.6点；POS层使达到同等标注准确率所需数据量减半以上。（2）全自动流水线中此增益尚未实现：自动标注器仍有12%的形态素标注错误，错误的POS比完全无POS更误导glossing模型。（3）噪声模拟显示，随着标注器准确率提升，增益逐步回归：在当前88%准确率附近达到盈亏平衡点，在92-96%时可回收+1.6至+3.2点增益。

Conclusion: 在濒危语言文档化实践中，推荐采用四行标注规范（文本、POS、注解、翻译），因为POS层蕴含的价值是潜在的而非丧失的——随着自动标注技术进步，该增益可被逐步回收。这一发现对有限数据预算下的语言档案化工作具有直接的指导意义。

Abstract: Discourse data are the primary empirical basis of grammar writing in field linguistics, but producing interlinearized text is notoriously expensive - on the order of one hour of work per minute of recording. For endangered languages, where the time remaining to verify analyses with native speakers is itself limited, automating parts of the interlinearization workflow has direct documentary value. We implement a full neural annotation pipeline (morpheme segmentation, POS tagging, glossing) for Irabu Ryukyuan using deliberately small, transparent BiLSTM-CRF models, and evaluate it under a realistic hard constraint: approximately one hour of fully annotated discourse as the entire supervised resource. Two factors of the annotation itself are manipulated: its richness (with or without a POS tier) and its quantity (training budgets from 6 to 47 minutes). Gold POS improves grammatical glossing by +4.4 (SD 0.7) points (significant in all 5 seeds), and the gain grows as data shrink (+11.6 points at a quarter of the data); a POS tier more than halves the amount of glossed data needed to reach a given accuracy. In a fully automatic pipeline this gain is not yet realized: the tagger still errs on 12% of morphemes, and an incorrect POS misleads the glossing model more than no POS at all. The value is latent rather than lost: degrading gold POS with controlled noise shows the gain returning as tagger accuracy rises, with break-even near our tagger's current 88% and +1.6 to +3.2 points recovered at 92-96%. We conclude with a concrete recommendation for documentation practice: annotate quadrilinearly - text, POS, gloss, translation.

</details>


### [102] [GFlowRL: Scaling Distribution-Matching RL to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13394)
*Xiaodong Liu,Michael Xu,Jack W. Stokes,Paul Smolensky,Doug Burger,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GFlowRL 是一种简化的 GFlowNet 风格强化学习算法，它移除了学习到的配分函数网络，改用批次内蒙特卡洛估计，并结合重要性采样修正和不对称流间隙裁剪，在数学、代码和对抗性红队测试中超越了所有对比方法，并首次在密集和稀疏架构上实现了稳定扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNets 通过匹配奖励分布来鼓励多样化推理路径，但扩展到现代后训练管线时，学习到的 prompt 条件配分函数会随模型规模、rollout 长度、奖励噪声和分布式系统复杂度的增加而成为梯度不稳定和工程开销的来源。

Method: 提出 GFlowRL 算法：用 rollout 组内的批次蒙特卡洛估计替代辅助配分网络，保留奖励分布匹配目标；加入两个稳定器——针对 rollout/训练器漂移的重要性采样修正，以及针对异常残差的非对称流间隙裁剪。

Result: 在 14B 规模下 Codeforces 评分达 2048（与 o3-mini 相差不到 25 Elo）；在 AdvBench 和 HarmBench 上取得最高平均 ASR@1；在 235B 参数的 MoE 配置上成功迁移，而 FlowRL 则发散。

Conclusion: GFlowRL 是首个在密集和稀疏架构上均能稳定扩展的 GFlowNet 风格 RL 算法，证明了配分函数可被简单估计替代，为大规模推理模型的后训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) offer a promising alternative to reward-maximizing reinforcement learning (RL) for large reasoning models, encouraging diverse reasoning paths by matching reward distributions rather than collapsing to dominant modes. Recent work shows promise on math and code, but scaling GFlowNet-style RL to modern post-training pipelines remains difficult: as model size, rollout horizon, reward noise, and distributed-systems complexity grow together, a learned prompt-conditional partition function becomes a source of gradient instability and engineering overhead rather than a useful normalizer. Through systematic analysis, we find that the learned partition function, previously treated as essential, can be replaced by an in-batch Monte Carlo estimate computed from the rollout group already required for training. We propose GFlowRL, a streamlined GFlowNet-style RL algorithm that removes the auxiliary partition network entirely while preserving the reward-distribution-matching objective, completed by two stabilizers: importance-sampling correction for rollout/trainer drift and asymmetric flow-gap clipping for outlier residuals. GFlowRL exceeds all counterparts on math, code, and adversarial red-teaming benchmarks, reaching a Codeforces rating of 2048 at the 14B scale (within 25 Elo of o3-mini) and attaining the highest average ASR@1 on AdvBench and HarmBench, outperforming the previous SOTA multi-turn attacker in a regime where FlowRL, a prior GFlowNet-style method, diverges. The same recipe transfers to all evaluated MoE configurations up to 235B parameters, where FlowRL again fails to converge. To our knowledge, GFlowRL is the first GFlowNet-style RL algorithm to scale stably across both dense and sparse architectures. Code will be at: https://github.com/microsoft/gflowrl

</details>


### [103] [Demystifying On-Policy Distillation: Roles, Pathologies, and Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13399)
*Rui Wang,Hongru Wang,Yi Chen,Boyang Xue,Tianqing Fang,Wenhao Yu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在LLM后训练中的动力学机制，揭示了OPD作为探索催化剂的作用，诊断了学生-教师不匹配和长度利用两种病理现象，并提出优势裁剪和对数压缩等轻量级信号调控方法以解决这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在线策略蒸馏已成为LLM后训练的关键范式，但其训练动力学机制尚不清楚。作者旨在系统阐明OPD的作用、病理及调控方法，以填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过系统实验分析，作者首先验证OPD作为探索催化剂的作用（对比提示多样性与单题采样数的影响），随后诊断两种病理：学生-教师不匹配（分布差距导致引导信号与任务正确性错位）和长度利用（token级目标聚合产生长度依赖捷径）。最后提出优势裁剪和对数压缩两种轻量级信号调控方法，在七个基准上验证其有效性。

Result: 实验表明：(1)提示多样性比单题采样数更重要；(2)OPD有效性完全取决于引导信号质量；(3)优势裁剪和对数压缩可缓解长度利用，实现有效蒸馏，稳定超越OPD变体和RLVR基线。

Conclusion: 良好调控的信号质量——而非单纯的教师模型规模——是OPD中成功探索的决定性因素。轻量级信号调控即可有效驯服OPD的病理现象，实现稳定且高效的在线策略蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a key paradigm in LLM post-training, yet its training dynamics remain poorly understood. We present a systematic study examining the role, pathologies, and regulations of OPD. We first clarify the role of OPD as an exploration catalyst: it steers the student toward correct reasoning paths via dense token-level guidance, without expanding capability ceiling. We confirm this by showing that prompt diversity matters more than per-problem sampling numbers, and critically, that the effectiveness of OPD hinges entirely on the quality of its guiding signal. This dependency exposes two pathologies that derail exploration. The Student-Teacher Mismatch occurs when a large teacher-student distributional gap causes the guiding signal to misalign with task correctness, steering exploration in counterproductive directions. Length Exploitation arises when the aggregated token-level objective creates length-dependent shortcuts, allowing the student to game the reward landscape through response truncation or redundant padding, exploring degenerate length modes rather than reasoning strategies. To tame these pathologies, we investigate lightweight signal regulations: advantage clipping and log-scale compression, ensuring exploration is guided by faithful signals. Experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate that these regulations alleviate length exploitation and enable effective distillation, stably surpassing OPD variants and RLVR baselines, thereby confirming that well-regulated signal quality, rather than mere teacher scale, governs successful exploration in OPD.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring Post-Training Alignment of Small Language Models for Biomedical Data-to-Text Generation: A Case Study of Medication Leaflet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13430)
*Xi Yang,Guodong Liu,Chuqin Li,Fan Wu,Ergin Soysal,Min Jiang,Xing He,Jiang Bian,Yi Guo,Shams Zaman,Thomas Fuchs,Todd Sanger,Yonghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了多种训练后对齐方法（SFT、DPO、ORPO、GRPO）在生物医学数据到文本生成任务上的表现，发现对齐后的小语言模型可超越GPT-5等闭源模型，其中ORPO优于SFT基线，GRPO跨数据集泛化能力最稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将复杂生物医学数据转化为患者友好叙述是生物医学信息学的核心需求。然而，当前缺乏对不同训练后对齐方法在生物医学数据到文本生成领域中小语言模型上的系统比较研究。

Method: 基于Qwen系列小语言模型，在药品说明书数据集上对比四种训练后方法：监督微调（SFT）、直接偏好优化（DPO）、比值偏好优化（ORPO）和分组相对策略优化（GRPO）。同时从openFDA收集药品标签数据以评估跨数据集泛化能力，使用ROUGE等词汇重叠指标和语义相似度指标进行评估。

Result: （1）对齐后的小语言模型性能超越GPT-5等闭源模型；（2）ORPO在所有实验中均优于SFT基线；（3）GRPO在跨数据集泛化方面表现最稳健，同样优于GPT-5。

Conclusion: 训练后对齐方法能显著提升小语言模型在生物医学数据到文本生成任务上的表现，ORPO是比SFT更优的选择，而GRPO在需要跨数据集泛化的场景下更具优势。

Abstract: Translating complex biomedical data into patient-friendly narratives is central to modern biomedical informatics. This study presents a comparative analysis of training small language models (SLMs) in specialized biomedical datato-text generation tasks. We explore widely adopted post-training methods including supervised fine-tuning (SFT), direct preference optimization (DPO), odds ratio preference optimization (ORPO), and group relative policy optimization (GRPO) with Qwen-based SLMs on a medicine package leaflets dataset. To assess cross-dataset generalizability, we also curated drug label data from openFDA. We evaluate models using both standard lexical overlap metrics like ROUGE as well as semantic similarity measures. Across our experiments, the results show that (1) the aligned SLMs outperform proprietary models like GPT-5; (2) ORPO outperforms the SFTbaselines; (3) GRPO yields the most robust cross-dataset performance among the alignment methods tested as well as GPT-5.

</details>


### [105] [When Rubrics Change: Cross-Rubric Generalization for Critical Thinking Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13433)
*Nischal Ashok Kumar,Payu Wittawatolarn,Sana Kang,Marisa C. Peczuh,Blair Lehman,Ryan Baker,Caitlin Mills,Sherry Lachman,Ruochen Sun,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自动化作文评分的跨评分标准泛化问题，提出基于LLM微调的框架，利用评分标准无关的中间表示（traits）和目标作文监督，在未见过的评分标准上显著提升了泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES研究多关注跨提示泛化（评分标准不变），但实际教学中评分标准常被修订或新增以评估不同能力维度。因此，亟需研究模型在未见过的评分标准下的泛化能力，即跨评分标准泛化。

Method: 采用LLM微调框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) rubric-agnostic中间表示，称为traits（特质），作为与具体评分标准解耦的中间层；(2) 在训练阶段利用已见评分标准下的目标作文监督信号。在包含多种批判性思维技能评分标签的AES数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在最困难的设置（目标评分标准和目标作文均未见过）下，traits使宏平均F1相比无traits基线提升5.0%。增加目标作文监督可进一步提升性能；最佳微调的开源Llama模型比GPT-5-mini提示高出2.1%宏F1，仅落后GPT-5约1.9%。

Conclusion: 基于trait的中间结构化表示与受控监督能有效改善模型对未见评分标准的泛化能力，为AES系统在实际教学场景中的灵活部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) research has largely focused on cross-prompt generalization, where essays from unseen prompts are scored while the scoring criteria are typically held constant. In practice, however, educators may revise or even introduce new rubrics in their scoring task, to evaluate different aspects of essays. We study cross-rubric generalization: training on essays labeled under one set of rubrics and evaluating on previously unseen rubrics, which target different aspects of the essay. We use a Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning framework with two components: rubric-agnostic intermediate representations, called traits, and target-essay supervision under seen rubrics during training. On an AES dataset augmented with multiple rubric-defined labels of student critical thinking skills, we find that traits improve macro F1 by 5.0% over a baseline without traits in the hardest setting, where both target rubrics and target essays are unseen during training. We further find that increasing target-essay supervision improves performance, with our best fine-tuned open-source Llama-based model outperforming GPT-5-mini prompting by 2.1% macro F1 and trailing GPT-5 by 1.9%. These results show that trait-based intermediate structure and controlled supervision improve generalization to unseen rubrics.

</details>


### [106] [Live Gurbani Tracking: A Benchmark and Reference System for Captioning Sikh Kirtan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13457)
*Karanbir Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了锡克教Kirtan实时字幕的基准与参考系统，将其形式化为闭集词汇的(shabad_id, line_idx)预测任务，按实时/离线与盲/先知两个维度组织问题空间，发布含12个评估案例的v1基准及参考系统，在live×blind最困难设置下达到57.9%帧准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kirtan颂唱的字幕显示必须是经书SGGS的精确逐字内容，拼写错误的Gurmukhi文本在宗教上是不被接受的。然而，通用ASR指标（WER/CER）衡量的是转录准确性而非显示准确性，因此需要一个专门针对该任务的基准和评估框架。

Method: 将任务定义为在每个时间步t预测(shabad_id, line_idx)或空值；构建2×2问题空间（live/offline × blind/oracle）；发布v1基准（4段手工标注录音×3种冷启动偏移=12个评估案例，约57分钟）；设计基于1秒分辨率的帧准确率评分器（含1秒容差和段边界间隙容忍）；参考系统采用微调的120M IndicConformer → 模糊匹配器 → 状态机，INT8 ONNX量化，在单核Apple Silicon上RTF约0.05。

Result: 在live×blind最困难变体上，参考系统在全部12个案例中达到57.9%总体帧准确率（12个中10个正确锁定shabad_id），并与三个平凡基线（空输出、偏移5秒、完美）进行了对比。

Conclusion: 提出了首个针对闭集词汇宗教颂唱实时字幕的基准与系统，论证了标准ASR指标不适用于该场景，所发布的基准、参考系统及实时部署均以宽松许可开源，为后续改进奠定基础。

Abstract: We present a benchmark and reference system for live captioning of Sikh Kirtan - the continuous, sung recitation of verses from the Sri Guru Granth Sahib Ji (SGGS). Unlike open-vocabulary lyrics transcription, Kirtan captioning is a closed-vocabulary problem: every displayed line must be an exact, word-for-word line from the canonical scripture, because displaying misspelled Gurmukhi is considered religiously inappropriate. We formalize the task as predicting, at every time t, a pair (shabad_id, line_idx) or null, and organize the problem space into a 2x2 matrix along two orthogonal axes: live vs. offline (causal vs. full-audio access) and blind vs. oracle (shabad identity discovered vs. given). We release v1 of the benchmark - 4 hand-annotated Kirtan recordings x 3 cold-start offsets = 12 evaluation cases, ~57 minutes of scored audio - together with a scorer that computes frame accuracy at 1s resolution over a scored region, with a 1s collar and gap-tolerant scoring at segment boundaries. We describe a reference system (fine-tuned 120M IndicConformer -> fuzzy matcher -> state machine; INT8 ONNX; RTF ~0.05 on one Apple Silicon core) that achieves 57.9% overall frame accuracy across all 12 cases (10/12 correct shabad locks) on the hardest variant (live x blind). We compare against three trivial baselines (empty, shifted-5s, perfect) and discuss why standard ASR metrics (WER/CER) measure transcription accuracy rather than the display accuracy this task requires. The benchmark, reference system, and a live deployment are released under permissive licenses to facilitate further improvements.

</details>


### [107] [DevicesWorld: Benchmarking Cross-Device Agents in Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13465)
*Huatao Li,Xinwei Geng,Yuheng Wang,Yutong Li,Runde Yang,Hantao Chen,Shu Yao,Jingru Fan,Xuhui Ren,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DevicesWorld 是一个大规模跨设备协作操作的基准测试，包含 6140 个任务，覆盖移动端、桌面端和物联网三类环境。当前最先进 LLM 智能体在该基准上最高成功率仅 12.5%，揭示了跨设备信息获取、整合与联合条件满足等方面的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实用户目标常跨越多个设备（手机获取信息、桌面处理、结果输出到另一设备），但现有基准集中在单一执行环境，无法评估智能体跨异构设备获取和整合信息的能力。因此需要构建统一的跨设备交互与评估框架。

Method: 提出 DevicesWorld：一个可执行的大规模跨设备基准，集成移动端、桌面端、IoT 三类设备环境。包含 6140 个任务，每个任务定义自然语言用户目标、参与设备及初始状态、可执行动作、基于规则的验证器和清理流程。采用多阶段构建与质量控制流水线，使任务贴近真实需求，并通过设备状态和生成文件自动验证结果。在此基准上评估了五个前沿 LLM 智能体系统。

Result: 五个前沿 LLM 智能体系统成功率均很低，最佳仅 12.5%。约 28.7% 的失败运行至少满足一个评分条件但仍未完成完整任务。轨迹分析显示智能体在信息获取或界面操控中陷入停滞、混淆源设备与目标设备，或在所有条件联合满足前提前终止。

Conclusion: DevicesWorld 将跨设备协作操作转化为可执行、可复现且具有诊断价值的评估问题，为可靠的跨设备智能体研究提供了重要基础，同时揭示了当前方法在多设备协同方面的显著不足。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have rapidly improved at operating individual digital environments such as mobile applications, desktop systems, and smart homes. However, real-world user goals often span multiple devices: information may come from a phone, be processed on a desktop, and the result may need to appear on another device. Most existing benchmarks center on a single dominant execution environment, making it difficult to evaluate whether agents can acquire and integrate information across heterogeneous devices and complete end-to-end tasks with cross-device dependencies. We introduce DevicesWorld, a large-scale executable benchmark for cross-device collaborative operation. DevicesWorld contains 6,140 tasks and integrates three classes of device environments -- mobile, desktop, and IoT -- into a unified cross-device interaction and evaluation framework. Each task defines a natural-language user goal, participating devices and initial states, executable actions, rule-based verifiers, and a cleanup procedure. A multi-stage construction and quality-control pipeline keeps tasks close to realistic user needs while allowing final outcomes to be automatically verified from device states and generated files. We evaluate five frontier LLM-agent systems on a fixed evaluation set. All methods achieve low success rates, with the best reaching only 12.5%. Among failed runs, about 28.7% satisfy at least one scoring condition yet still fail the full task. Trajectories show that agents become stuck acquiring information or manipulating interfaces, confuse source and output devices, or terminate before all conditions are jointly satisfied. DevicesWorld turns cross-device collaborative operation into an executable, reproducible, and diagnostically useful evaluation problem for research on reliable cross-device agents.

</details>


### [108] [MyAG: A Graph-Based Framework for Designing and Analyzing Composable LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13474)
*Zhisong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MyAG，一个基于图抽象的框架，将 LLM Agent 系统拆分为组件图、工作流图和搜索图，支持灵活组合、层次化构建及性能可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 框架在构建复杂系统时，组件复用性差、执行策略耦合紧密，缺乏对性能-效率权衡的系统化分析工具。

Method: MyAG 采用三层图抽象：组件图定义 Agent、环境和模块；工作流图控制执行流程；搜索图管理运行时执行。支持递归系统节点实现层次化组合，并提供监控与可视化工具。

Result: 在代表性 Agent 应用上的实验表明，MyAG 能灵活支持多种 Agent 系统设计，并有效辅助分析性能与效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: MyAG 通过三层图抽象实现了组件与策略的解耦，为组合式 LLM Agent 系统的设计、分析与优化提供了通用且开源的基础设施。

Abstract: We present MyAG, a graph-based framework for designing and analyzing composable LLM agent systems. Our framework separates agent system construction into three graph abstractions: a component graph for agents, environments, and modules; a workflow graph for execution control; and a search graph for runtime execution. This separation allows users to flexibly reuse the same components with different strategies. We further support hierarchical composition through recursive system nodes and provide monitoring and visualization tools for inspecting agent execution. Experiments on representative agent applications show that our framework supports flexible agent system design and helps analyze performance-efficiency tradeoffs. Our framework is publicly available and fully open-source.

</details>


### [109] [Cost-Pragmatic Quality Gating and Selection-Fusion Multi-Model Combiners for BioASQ Phases A+ and B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13551)
*Dima Galat,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个BioASQ问答系统，核心围绕两个设计决策：弱第一阶段检索时的重检索策略，以及多模型答案的融合方法。系统采用双管道检索（混合第一阶段+智能体驱动管道），配合质量门控进行选择性重检索，并使用LLM-as-judge与同义词联合解析器进行多模型集成，在BioASQ 14B排行榜上取得多项第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对生物医学问答中第一阶段检索质量波动的问题，需要设计高效的重检索策略以在成本与性能间取得平衡；同时，多语言模型答案融合缺乏系统性理解，需要解构集成提升的来源，以指导融合策略的选择。

Method: (1) 检索：采用双管道设计——混合第一阶段检索（稠密BGE + BM25 + RRF融合，历史数据R@200达99.3%）与智能体驱动管道（将问题分解后在PubMed、Europe PMC、iCite上检索），通过BGE交叉编码器质量门控标记弱支持问题以触发选择性重检索。(2) 融合：将多模型集成提升分解为选择组件（受单问题oracle约束）和融合组件（聚合器可超越），在验证集上对比LLM-as-judge与同义词联合解析器的表现。

Result: (1) 成本务实重检索策略在Task 12B验证集上显著优于技能严格基线，list F1和list precision提升，重检索成本降低12%。(2) 固定提示和模型下，Task 13B的list F1绝对值提升+0.132。(3) 融合分析表明LLM-as-judge在选择主导指标（yes/no、多参考ROUGE）上获胜，但在融合友好指标的召回分量上结构不足；同义词联合解析器在所有头部上赢得list recall，GPT-5.5独奏保持list-F1领先。(4) 在Task 14B 2026初步排行榜上，在八个（阶段×批次）排行榜中三个综合精确聚合排名第一，赢得四个单独问题类型单元，并在Phase B b3 ideal上获#1。

Conclusion: 选择性重检索与多模型融合的协同设计能有效提升生物医学问答系统性能。检索端仍有显著提升空间，而融合策略应根据指标类型（选择主导 vs. 融合友好）进行针对性选择，不存在单一通用最优方案。

Abstract: We describe our BioASQ Task 14B 2026 system. The work centers on two design decisions: how aggressively to re-retrieve when first-stage retrieval is weak, and how to combine multiple language-model answers. Retrieval unions two parallel pipelines - a hybrid first stage (dense BGE + BM25 + RRF, reaching R@200 = 99.3% on the BioASQ-13b historical archive) and an agent-driven pipeline that decomposes the question over PubMed, Europe PMC, and iCite - with a BGE cross-encoder quality gate flagging weakly-supported questions for selective re-retrieval. On Task 12B 2024 validation, a cost-pragmatic re-retrieval policy beats a skill-strict baseline significantly on list F1 and list precision, at 12% lower re-retrieval cost. Holding prompt and model fixed across val and test 13B (different question sets), list F1 rises by +0.132 absolute on the BioASQ-released gold-input pool, consistent with substantial retrieval-side headroom. For Phase B answering we decompose multi-model ensemble lift into a selection component bounded by the per-question oracle and a fusion component that aggregators can exceed. The decomposition predicts before any experiment that LLM-as-judge wins on selection-dominated metrics (yes/no, multi-reference ROUGE) but is structurally insufficient on the recall component of fusion-friendly metrics (factoid rank-1, list recall). On Task 13B 2025 our synonym-union resolver wins list recall on every head, while GPT-5.5 solo retains the list-F1 lead because the resolver's wider item set costs precision. On the Task 14B 2026 preliminary leaderboard our team places first on the combined-exact aggregate on three of the eight (phase x batch) leaderboards, wins four individual question-type cells, and takes #1 on Phase B b3 ideal.

</details>


### [110] [Graded Entity-Familiarity Readouts in Language Models: Polish Adaptation, Cross-Language Robustness, and Refusal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13568)
*Grzegorz Brzezinka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了12个指令微调模型在实体熟悉度探测上的表现,发现最终提示token的激活可以区分真实/虚构实体,且探针分数与实体流行度相关,尤其在波兰语适配模型中更显著;熟悉度方向可操控拒答行为,熟悉度探针可作为生成前拒答门控的竞争性方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否能在生成答案之前评估自身对实体的熟悉程度,从而支持更可靠的拒答/弃权机制。当前对模型内部表征中实体熟悉度信号的存在性、跨语言鲁棒性及与拒答策略的关系尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: (1)构建包含1440个波兰实体(涵盖4个领域、10个维基百科页面浏览量分位)及虚构对照的新数据集；(2)在Bielik、PLLuM、Gemma-4、Qwen3四个家族共12个指令微调模型上提取最终提示token的激活；(3)训练线性探针检测熟悉度信号,评估AUROC及与实体流行度的Spearman秩相关；(4)进行跨语言鲁棒性实验(波兰语/英语提示切换)；(5)在Gemma-4-12B上通过添加一维熟悉度方向操控拒答率；(6)对比生成前熟悉度探针与生成后检测器作为弃权门控的效果。

Result: 所有模型家族中熟悉度探针均可区分真实与虚构实体；波兰语适配的Bielik和PLLuM家族中探针分数与实体流行度显著相关(Spearman ρ 0.28-0.57),而Gemma-4和Qwen3最多0.11,该模式与波兰语适配而非参数量更相关；跨语言切换提示后探针AUROC保持96-101%；在Gemma-4-12B中添加熟悉度方向可使已知实体拒答率从0.24升至1.00,未知实体从0.73降至0.00；校准后的熟悉度探针在生成前弃权门控中具有竞争力,但生成后检测器平均更能预测行为错误。

Conclusion: 模型在生成前即存在分级的实体熟悉度读出信号,且表征层面的熟悉度与将其转化为拒答行为的策略机制是可分离的。这为构建更可靠的模型弃权机制提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Can a language model estimate its familiarity with an entity before generating an answer? We study activations at the final prompt token in twelve instruction-tuned models from the Bielik, PLLuM, Gemma-4, and Qwen3 families, using a new dataset of 1,440 Polish entities spanning four domains and ten Wikipedia-pageview deciles, plus fabricated controls. Familiarity-probe scores separate real from fabricated entities in every family; in the Polish-adapted Bielik and PLLuM families they additionally track entity popularity (model-mean Spearman $ρ$ 0.28-0.57, versus at most 0.11 in Gemma-4 and Qwen3), a pattern more strongly associated with Polish adaptation than with parameter count in this model sample. In a paired experiment on two families, probes retain 96-101% of within-language AUROC when the Polish question stem is replaced with an English one around unchanged entity names, showing robustness to prompt language in this setting. In Gemma-4-12B, the only model that natively refuses, adding a one-dimensional familiarity direction at a single layer moves refusal rates monotonically in both directions (0.24 to 1.00 on well-known entities; 0.73 to 0.00 on unknown ones). Finally, a calibrated familiarity probe is competitive among pre-generation abstention gates, although post-generation detectors better predict behavioral error on average. These results support a graded pre-generation entity-familiarity readout, and a separation between representational familiarity and the policy that converts it into abstention.

</details>


### [111] [Memory as a Controlled Process: Learned Adaptive Memory Management for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13591)
*Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Yuchen Wu,Levina Li,Dong Liu,Xiao Liang,Rui Sun,Yubei Li,Edward Sun,Haozheng Luo,Zhaolu Kang,Aylin Caliskan,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemCon将记忆操作建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过在线学习的上下文Bandit策略自适应地决定检索、注入、整合与遗忘的时机和方式，无需预训练或额外LLM调用，在多个基准上显著提升任务成功率并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的外部记忆系统均采用固定的手工启发式规则来访问记忆，但最优记忆行为本质上是上下文依赖的——任务早期应减少检索（记忆稀疏），重复目标类型应复用计划而非通用检索，卡住时应更换查询重新检索，长期任务流中需整合修剪记忆。这种静态记忆观成为智能体学习的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出MemCon框架，将记忆操作建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），使用轻量级表格化上下文Bandit（UCB探索）在线学习自适应策略，决定：(1)何时、检索什么、检索多少；(2)何时注入精炼计划；(3)何时整合或遗忘。该框架后端无关，可封装任何现有记忆实现，仅需任务级二元反馈，无需预训练和额外LLM调用，数十个任务内即可收敛。

Result: 在6个基准、3个智能体框架和3个LLM骨干上，MemCon一致优于多种记忆基线方法，任务成功率最高提升15.2个百分点，同时token消耗降低5–20%。

Conclusion: 记忆应被视为受控过程而非静态存储，通过自适应在线策略学习可显著突破固定启发式记忆访问的瓶颈，MemCon为此提供了通用、轻量、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory systems to accumulate experience across tasks. Yet nearly all existing approaches, from graph-structured memories to reflective insight stores, access memory through fixed, hand-designed heuristics. We argue that this static view of memory is a core bottleneck for agentic learning because optimal memory behavior is fundamentally context-dependent. The early stages of the tasks, benefit from minimal retrieval because memory is sparse; recurring goal types benefit from plan reuse rather than generic nearest-neighbor lookup; stuck agents benefit from re-retrieval with alternative queries; and across long task streams, the memory store itself must be consolidated and pruned to remain useful. We present Memory as a Controlled Process (MemCon), a framework that models memory operations as a Markov Decision Process and learns an online policy that adaptively decides when, what, and how much to retrieve, when to inject a distilled plan, and when to consolidate or forget. MemCon is backend-agnostic: it wraps any existing memory implementation, learns from task-by-task binary feedback with no pretraining and no additional LLM calls, and uses a lightweight tabular contextual bandit with UCB exploration that converges within tens of tasks. Across 6 benchmarks, 3 agent frameworks, and 3 LLM backbones, MemCon consistently outperforms multiple memory baselines by up to 15.2 points in task success while reducing token consumption by 5--20%.

</details>


### [112] [Self-Evolving Agent Harnesses via Gated Semantic Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13683)
*Xiaotian Luo,Fengxingyu Wang,Chuanrui Hu,Dizhan Xue,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个自演化的智能体框架（harness）自动优化系统，通过将"提议变更"与"归因验证"分离，结合确定性统计检验和基于病理特征的归档机制（GSME），在冻结模型权重下实现跨七个领域+9至+15.5pp的可信性能提升，且增益具有良好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的实际性能在很大程度上取决于模型外部的框架组件（提示词、注入知识、运行时控制、配置），而这往往是部署中唯一可调的部分。然而，自动改进框架的核心难点不在于生成变更，而在于判断哪些变更真正有效——自生成的反馈噪声大，表面增益可能是测量伪影或对调优任务的过拟合。因此需要一种能可信地归因改进的方法。

Method: 提出自演化智能体框架，核心设计是将"提议变更"与"归因验证"解耦：语言模型负责诊断失败并提议补丁，而所有采样、测量和显著性检验由确定性代码完成，确保每个被认可的改进在构造上就是可信的。补丁被归档到一个带门控的、基于类别的质量-多样性档案（GSME）中，归档键为补丁所解决的(WHERE × WHY)病理特征，而非其修复的具体任务，这是一种反过拟合的归纳偏置。泛化性通过在演化结束后才评分的密封测试集来衡量。

Result: 在七个领域、使用冻结开源权重模型的实验中，框架在训练集上被选择后在密封测试集上一次性评分，可信增益达+9至+15.5个百分点，且保留了训练增益的86%至147%，表明改进具有泛化性而非过拟合。胜出的补丁追踪的是模型的主导病理特征，而非模型的规模或家族：更换模型会改变病理特征和相应补丁，但同一病理到补丁的匹配关系在两个模型家族中反复出现。可迁移的是"诊断-归因"循环本身，而非具体的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将病理诊断、确定性验证和反过拟合归档机制有机结合，实现了对LLM智能体框架组件的可信自动优化。实验证明该方法产生的性能提升具有跨任务的泛化性，且诊断-归因循环本身可跨模型迁移，为部署场景下无需修改模型权重的性能提升提供了可靠路径。

Abstract: An LLM agent's real-task performance is shaped as much by the harness around its model as by the frozen model itself: its prompts, injected knowledge, runtime control, and configuration. In deployment the harness is often the only lever available, so improving it automatically is the natural way to raise performance without touching the weights. The hard part is not generating changes but knowing which one truly helped. Self-generated feedback is noisy, and an apparent gain can be a measurement artifact or an edit that merely overfits the tasks it was tuned on. We present a self-evolving agent-harness framework that separates proposing changes from crediting them: a language model diagnoses failures and proposes patches, while all sampling, measurement, and significance testing are owned by deterministic code, so every credited improvement is trustworthy by construction. Patches populate a gated, categorical quality-diversity archive (GSME) keyed on the (WHERE x WHY) pathology an edit addresses rather than the tasks it fixes, an anti-overfitting inductive bias; generalization is measured on a sealed test scored only after evolution. Across seven domains with a frozen open-weight model, the harness is train-selected and scored once on a sealed test; its credited gains there are +9 to +15.5pp and retain 86-147% of the training gain, evidence they generalize rather than overfit. The winning patch tracks the model's dominant pathology, not its size or family: changing the model can change the pathology and the patch, while the same pathology-to-patch match recurs across two model families. What transfers is the diagnose-and-credit loop, not any specific harness.

</details>


### [113] [The Test Oracle Problem in Synthetic LLM-as-Judge Corpora: Disappearance, Distortion and a Validation Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13707)
*Serkan Ballı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了LLM-as-judge评估系统中一个隐蔽的结构性故障：生成合成语料库时，解码预算参数在生成和评判环节间共享，导致幻觉答案被截断。该故障造成了跨语言选择准确率32个百分点的虚假崩溃，且只能通过人工阅读原始生成文本发现。作者将其归结为"测试预言机问题"，并提出了针对无预言机语料库的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge系统的偏差研究通常依赖LLM生成的合成语料，但这些语料缺乏自动验证完整性的机制。作者通过亲身经历的一个隐蔽故障案例，揭示这一方法论存在结构性而非偶然性的脆弱点，并呼吁建立验证协议。

Method: 1. 案例剖析：在多语言（土耳其语/英语）忠实度判断语料库构建中，生成与评判调用共享的解码预算参数截断了幻觉答案，导致虚假的32点跨语言准确率崩塌。2. 对照实验：通过受控的生产者交换实验验证该效应为伪影。3. 正控制实验：将同类故障注入基于确定性扰动构建的语料库，用零成本的字符串比对即可100%捕获。4. 提出验证协议：针对无预言机语料库的分析工作流。

Result: 1. 共享参数故障造成的32点准确率崩塌在N=50到N=500均可复现，修复参数后效应消失至天花板。2. 另一个偏差（Markdown格式偏好）虽非伪造，但被同一故障扭曲，其效应大小和符号随刺激长度变化。3. 聚合统计指标无法区分上述两种偏差模式，仅人工阅读原始生成可暴露故障。4. 确定性扰动语料库中，零成本字符串比对以100%准确率捕获了同类故障。

Conclusion: LLM生成负样本的语料库面临"测试预言机问题"——缺乏机械方式验证项目完整性；而通过对金标准答案进行确定性扰动构建的语料库天然附带项目级预言机。作者认为当代多语言LLM-as-judge语料库大多处于无预言机状态，并提出了基于自身案例的验证协议供分析者使用。

Abstract: Studies of bias in LLM-as-judge systems typically build synthetic corpora by prompting an LLM to generate a hallucinated answer to pair with a factual one, then presenting both to a judge. We report a case in which this generation step silently failed, and use it to argue that the failure mode is structural rather than incidental. In a multilingual (Turkish/English) faithfulness-judgment corpus, a decoding-budget parameter shared between judging and generation calls truncated one producer's hallucinated answers to a few words. The resulting items produced a large, statistically robust effect: a 32-point cross-lingual collapse in one judge's selection accuracy, replicated from N=50 to N=500, explained by a three-layer mechanistic account, and confirmed by a controlled producer-swap experiment, none of which was real. The effect vanished to ceiling once the shared parameter was corrected, and only manual reading of the raw generations, not any aggregate statistical check, exposed the fault. A second measured bias (Markdown-formatting preference) was not fabricated but distorted by the same fault, its magnitude and in one case its sign shifting with stimulus length, a mode aggregate metrics cannot distinguish from the first. We frame the underlying vulnerability using the test oracle problem: corpora whose negative examples are LLM-generated carry no mechanical way to verify item integrity, while corpora built by deterministic perturbation of a gold answer carry an item-level oracle for free. A positive control supports this claim directly: an analogous fault injected into a minimal perturbation-based corpus is caught with 100% accuracy by a zero-cost, zero-human gold-to-negative string comparison. We close with a validation protocol, derived from our own case, for analysts working in the oracle-less regime that we argue describes most contemporary multilingual LLM-as-judge corpora.

</details>


### [114] [Self-supervised Speech Comparison for L2 Phone, Rhythm, and Intonation Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13721)
*Stephen McIntosh,Reuben Smit,Daisuke Saito,Nobuaki Minematsu,Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督WavLM表征与动态时间规整(DTW)的文本无关框架，用于同时评估L2语音的发音准确性、节奏和语调三个韵律维度，在英语和日语数据上取得了接近甚至超越人类评分一致性的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L2语音评估侧重于音素层面的发音评估，对节奏、语调等超音段特征的自动评分研究不足；同时现有方法依赖标注的L2语音数据进行训练，难以在低资源场景下应用。因此需要一种无需文本标注、可多维度评估发音质量的方法。

Method: 使用预训练的WavLM自监督模型提取语音表征，通过DTW将学习者语音与母语者模板进行对齐：(1) 发音准确性：直接用DTW距离进行评分；(2) 节奏：通过测量DTW对齐路径的扭曲程度来量化节奏偏差；(3) 语调：在韵律残差上计算DTW距离，并结合基频(F0)和强度特征。

Result: (1) 发音评分：基础DTW方法在整体和句子级别的语音评分上超越了人类评分者间的一致性；(2) 节奏评分：所提出的扭曲度测量方法接近人类水平；(3) 语调评分：结合韵律残差DTW距离与F0/强度特征的方法在某些任务上表现尚可，但整体效果较为有限。

Conclusion: 自监督语音表征为多维度发音质量评估提供了一种有前景的文本无关基础方案，尤其在高资源场景下的发音和节奏评估表现优异，但语调评估仍有提升空间。

Abstract: L2 speech assessment has traditionally focused on phonetic assessment, leaving the scoring of suprasegmental features such as rhythm and intonation underexplored. Moreover, assessment methods often require training with labeled L2 speech data, making them difficult to apply in low-resource settings. We investigate whether DTW over self-supervised WavLM representations can provide a text-free framework for assessing phonetic accuracy, rhythm, and intonation in English and Japanese L2 speech. Results show that a basic DTW-based approach that compares learner speech to native templates exceeds human agreement on holistic and sentence-level phonetic scoring. For rhythm, we introduce methods that measure the degree of warping in the DTW alignment path; our best method approaches human-level performance. For intonation, we combine DTW distance over prosodic residuals with pitch and intensity features, but performance remains more modest on some tasks. Our results point to self-supervised representations as a promising, text-free basis for multi-aspect pronunciation assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Post-Training Shifts Confidence: A Three-Stage Analysis of How SFT, RL, and OPD Shape Pre-, Intra-, and Post-CoT Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13753)
*Shuhao Li,Guodong Du,Anhao Zhao,Wanyu Lin,Tianyu Yuan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型后训练方法（SFT、RL、OPD）对推理过程中置信度校准的影响，提出三阶段校准框架，发现不同方法在不同推理阶段各有优势，并基于位置依赖性提出PosConf策略，显著提升了推理置信度的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法仅以最终答案准确率评估，缺乏对推理过程中置信度如何演变的系统研究。作者旨在揭示SFT、RL和OPD在推理前、中、后三个阶段的置信度特性，以优化推理效率与可靠性。

Method: 提出三阶段校准框架：推理前置信度（难度估计）、推理中置信度（早停决策）、推理后置信度（答案聚合）。在数学推理基准上进行受控对比实验，分析三种后训练方法的置信度特性。基于发现的位置依赖性——RL置信度在"路径承诺"后变得可靠，OPD早期有用但后期出现逆校准——提出PosConf策略，仅从可靠的相对位置区间提取置信度信号。

Result: OPD提供最优的推理前置信度；SFT给出最强的在线早停信号；RL产生最可靠的trace级别聚合信号。PosConf使RL答案聚合在多数投票基础上提升6.1个百分点，在严格token预算下OPD早停提升达4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 推理模型中的置信度应当同时从阶段维度（stage-wise）和位置维度（position-aware）加以利用，PosConf策略能有效避开逆校准区域，显著提升推理效率与准确性。

Abstract: Large language models have made strong reasoning gains through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation, yet these post-training methods are usually evaluated only by final-answer accuracy. We study how they reshape confidence during reasoning. We introduce a three-stage calibration framework that evaluates confidence before, during, and after chain-of-thought generation, corresponding to difficulty estimation, early termination, and answer aggregation. Through a controlled comparison on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we find that OPD provides the most useful pre-reasoning confidence, SFT gives the strongest online signal for early stopping, and RL produces the most reliable trace-level signal for aggregation. We further show that confidence reliability is position-dependent: RL confidence becomes informative after a path-commitment phase, while OPD confidence is useful early but can become inversely calibrated later. Based on this observation, we propose PosConf, a position-aware confidence strategy that uses confidence only from reliable relative-position intervals. PosConf improves RL answer aggregation by 6.1 points over majority voting and consistently improves OPD early stopping under tight token budgets, with gains up to 4.3 points by avoiding its later inverse-calibration region, showing that \emph{confidence in reasoning models should be used both stage-wise and position-awarely}. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Post-Training-Calibration.

</details>


### [116] [SPyCE: Skill-Policy Co-evolution for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13854)
*Ru Zhang,Weijie Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPyCE 提出技能-策略协同演化框架，将多模态推理轨迹蒸馏为可复用的分层技能库，技能库与策略在强化学习过程中闭环互促，在八个基准上一致超越 RL 基线和记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 方法将轨迹压缩为标量奖励，策略需从零学习工具使用模式；记忆方法虽保留经验但仅做测试时检索，不更新策略。核心洞见是：多模态推理轨迹应被蒸馏为与策略协同演化的可复用技能，而非仅作为奖励消耗或静态存储。

Method: 提出 SPyCE 框架：(1) 将轨迹蒸馏为分层技能库——执行技能捕获局部视觉操作，工作流技能编码高层工具编排先验；(2) 训练时策略模型以检索到的技能为条件指导 rollout，同时技能库利用策略生成的高价值 rollout 持续更新；(3) 形成闭环——更好的策略产出更好的技能，演化的技能库反过来为策略提供更强先验。

Result: 在八个基准上 SPyCE 一致超越 RL 基线和记忆基线；消融分析表明分层技能设计和协同演化机制均对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 技能-策略联合优化是构建强大多模态智能体的有前景范式，分层技能与协同演化二者缺一不可。

Abstract: Multimodal agents that think with images iteratively manipulate visual evidence and invoke tools across many steps. Existing reinforcement learning methods reduce trajectories to scalar rewards, forcing the policy to discover reusable tool-use patterns from scratch on every new task; memory-based alternatives retain past experience, yet they rely on test-time retrieval, without updating the policy to absorb reusable patterns from that experience. Our key insight is that multimodal reasoning trajectories should be distilled into reusable skills that co-evolve with the policy during training, rather than being consumed as rewards or retrieved from a static store. To this end, we propose SPyCE (Skill-Policy Co-evolution), a framework that distills trajectories into a hierarchical skill library and updates it throughout reinforcement learning. Execution skills capture local visual operations, while workflow skills encode high-level priors that orchestrate tool use. During training, the policy model conditions on retrieved skills to guide its rollouts, while the skill library evolves using valuable rollouts generated by the policy. This creates a closed loop in which improved policies yield better skills, and the evolving skill library, in turn, provides stronger priors for policy rollouts. Experiments across eight benchmarks demonstrate that SPyCE consistently outperforms both RL-based and memory-based baselines. Further analysis reveals that both the hierarchical skill design and the co-evolution mechanism are critical to our design. These results suggest joint skill-policy optimization as a promising paradigm for building capable multimodal agents.

</details>


### [117] [High-Order Question Generation in a Multilingual Educational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13901)
*Suna-Şeyma Uçar,Itziar Aldabe,Nora Aranberri,Orphée De Clercq*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言环境下，基于"主张-证据-推理"和"发散性提问"两种替代布鲁姆分类法的框架生成高阶问题。结果表明，虽然模型能有效生成三种语言的问题，但仅约一半的可回答问题被教师认定为高阶问题；两种替代框架能产生结构性和概念性多样的问题，可作为布鲁姆分类法的可行补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高阶问题生成研究过度依赖布鲁姆分类法框架，且仅局限于英语语境。教育实践中教师设计高阶问题存在困难，课堂普遍使用低阶问题，而大语言模型在此方面显示出潜力。研究旨在填补框架单一性和语言局限性的空白。

Method: 引入两种替代布鲁姆分类法的提示框架：主张-证据-推理（Claim-Evidence-Reasoning, CER）和发散性提问（Divergent Questioning），在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言的多语环境下，分别使用一个开源模型和一个闭源商业模型进行问题生成实验，并由教师对生成问题的可回答性及认知层级进行人工评估。

Result: 两个模型在三种语言中均能较有效地生成问题；但所有可回答问题中，仅约一半被教师认定为高阶问题。积极方面在于，两种替代框架生成了结构上和概念上均具多样性的问题，表明它们可以相互补充，为布鲁姆分类法提供可行的替代方案。

Conclusion: CER和发散性提问框架可作为布鲁姆分类法的有效替代方案，在大语言模型辅助下生成多语言的高阶问题。尽管模型生成高阶问题的精准度仍有提升空间（约50%），但框架的多样性为教育者提供了更丰富的问题设计路径。

Abstract: Critical thinking is a fundamental skill that helps learners move beyond simple memorization. One way to develop this skill is through high-order questioning. However, crafting such questions remains a challenge for educators, and classroom practices tend to rely on low-order questions. Large Language Models have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating high-order questions, especially when guided by prompts based on Bloom's Taxonomy. Yet, existing research has largely centered on this framework and focused only on English. This study addresses these gaps by introducing prompts grounded in two alternative frameworks: Claim-Evidence-Reasoning and Divergent Questioning within a multilingual context using Basque, Spanish, and English. Results indicate that while both an open-source and a proprietary model rather effectively generate questions in all three languages, only about half of the answerable questions are recognized by teachers as high-order. A positive finding is that the alternative frameworks produce structurally and conceptually varied questions, suggesting they could complement each other and provide viable alternatives to Bloom's Taxonomy.

</details>


### [118] [DeepStress: Stress-Testing Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13920)
*Ismael Rousseau,Geraldine Damnati,Frederic Bechet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DeepStress压力测试框架，通过可控合成环境替代检索模块，系统评估搜索智能体在不可信、不相关、不事实证据下的鲁棒性，揭示不同智能体处理不可靠信息的能力差异，并提出新的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体在多步问答中表现优异，但对低质量证据的鲁棒性研究不足。真实基准中此类情况少见，但实际应用中可能导致灾难性失败，因此需要系统性压力测试框架来评估和揭示这一脆弱性。

Method: 提出DeepStress框架：(1)用可控合成环境替换搜索智能体的检索模块；(2)从可信度（trustworthiness）、相关性（relevance）和事实性（factuality）三个维度操控文档质量；(3)在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus上测试多款搜索智能体；(4)提出新指标量化系统输出及参数化知识与检索知识之间的冲突交互。

Result: 不同搜索智能体在处理不可靠信息方面表现出显著差异；新指标能更好地记录系统行为及参数化知识与检索知识之间的冲突。

Conclusion: 搜索智能体对低质量证据的鲁棒性差异显著，DeepStress框架和新指标为评估和改进搜索智能体的可靠性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: While search agents demonstrate impressive capabilities in multi-step question answering, their robustness to poor-quality evidence remains under-explored. This phenomenon occurs rarely in realistic benchmarks but can lead to dramatic failure in real life applications. Therefore in this study we propose DeepStress, a stress testing framework that controls the frequency of challenging evidence by replacing the retrieval module of search agents with a controlled synthetic environment. We use this framework to control three dimensions that can affect document reliability: trustworthiness, relevance, and factuality. Testing several search agents on HotpotQA and BrowseCompPlus, we demonstrate that agents exhibit substantial differences in their ability to handle unreliable information and propose new metrics that better document systems outcomes as well as the interactions between conflicting parametric and retrieved knowledge.

</details>


### [119] [DeltaMerge-LowRes: Composing Language and Task Deltas for Low-Resource Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13967)
*Son Ha Xuan,Xuan-Bach Le,Phat T. Tran-Truong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在低资源场景下，将语言适应和任务适应分别训练、再于权重空间中进行组合的范式，提出ΔMergeLowRes方法，并设计了四种组合规则（加性、激活引导、稀疏感知及新颖的跨轴TIES）。在四种任务和四种非洲语言上的实验表明，跨轴TIES在摘要和问答任务上显著优于仅任务微调，稀疏感知合并则在分类校准误差上大幅改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将多语言编码器对新语言和新任务的适应融合在一次昂贵的联合微调中。作者希望解耦这两个维度，分别获取语言增量（Δ_L）和任务增量（Δ_T），然后在推理时通过权重空间组合，以降低低资源场景下的训练成本。

Method: 提出ΔMergeLowRes框架：(1) 从无标签单语文本学习语言增量Δ_L，从有标签英语数据学习任务增量Δ_T；(2) 设计四种组合规则——加性合并、激活引导合并、稀疏感知合并，以及创新的跨轴TIES（将TIES-Merging的修剪、符号选举、合并步骤从双任务轴适配到语言-任务双轴）；(3) 在四种任务家族和四种非洲语言上，对158个评估单元进行10,000次配对自举检验。

Result: (i) 跨轴TIES在3/4语言的摘要任务上以+4至+7 chrF胜出（chrF 18.59 vs. 仅任务13.80）；(ii) 问答任务F1提升+2.32，EM提升+2.91；(iii) 稀疏感知合并在保持宏F1持平的情况下，将分类任务期望校准误差（ECE）降低36%。组合规则显著影响合并模型保留、抑制和校准的内容。

Conclusion: 语言适应和任务适应可以在权重空间中解耦并有效组合；组合规则的选择对模型行为有实质性影响，跨轴TIES和稀疏感知合并分别在不同指标上表现最优。作者开源了全部实验记录。

Abstract: Adapting a multilingual encoder to a new language \emph{and} a new task with only a few hundred gold examples is a common low-resource NLP setting, yet the two axes are usually fused via an expensive language--task fine-tuning run. We ask whether they can instead be trained separately and recombined in weight space. \DeltaMergeLowRes{} learns a language delta $Δ_L$ from unlabeled monolingual text and a task delta $Δ_T$ from labeled English data, then composes them at inference under one of four rules: additive, activation-guided, sparsity-aware, and a novel \emph{cross-axis TIES}. The new rule adapts the TIES-Merging steps of trimming, sign election, and merging to the language and task axes rather than to two task axes. Holding $(Δ_L,Δ_T)$ fixed across rules on four task families and four African languages ($158$ evaluated cells, $10{,}000$-sample paired bootstrap per cell), we find: (i) cross-axis TIES wins summarisation on $3/4$ languages by $+4$ to $+7$ chrF (chrF $18.59$ vs.\ $13.80$ task-only); (ii) it improves QA F1 by $+2.32$ and EM by $+2.91$; and (iii) sparsity-aware merging cuts classification ECE by $36\%$ at parity macro-F1. The composition rule materially changes what the merged model preserves, suppresses, and calibrates. We release all JSON traces and a claim ledger.

</details>


### [120] [Constraint-Aware Counterfactual Editing for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13977)
*S M Rafiuddin,Vamsi Krishna Pavuluri,Atriya Sen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAVE-ABSA 提出了一个约束感知的验证编辑框架，通过定位目标方面的意见跨度、受控改写、修复模块及多层验证（方面级验证、语义相似度、AMR结构保持、编辑最小性、流畅性、矛盾检测），生成并验证方面级的反事实样本，用于ABSA模型的鲁棒性评估和数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实生成方法多关注句子级标签翻转，产生的编辑虽流畅但往往在方面级别无效、语义漂移或自相矛盾。ABSA需要针对特定方面的情感翻转，同时保持非目标方面的情感、语义、流畅性和事实一致性，现有方法难以满足这一约束。

Method: CAVE-ABSA 框架包含：(1) 定位目标方面关联的意见跨度；(2) 受控反事实改写；(3) 修复模块精炼候选；(4) 多层过滤验证——方面级验证、语义相似度、AMR引导的结构保持、编辑最小性、流畅性和矛盾检测。生成与验证显式分离。

Result: 该框架可构建经过验证的反事实ABSA数据集，用于鲁棒性评估和数据增强，能够测试ABSA模型是否真正依赖方面锚定的情感推理。

Conclusion: CAVE-ABSA 通过生成与验证分离的机制，提供了生成有意义、方面局部的反事实样本的原则性方法，为ABSA模型的鲁棒性测试和训练数据增强提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) requires models to identify sentiment toward specific aspects rather than relying on the global polarity of a sentence. This makes counterfactual evaluation especially challenging: a valid counterfactual should flip the sentiment of one target aspect while preserving the sentiment of all non-target aspects, semantic meaning, fluency, and factual consistency. Existing counterfactual generation methods often focus on sentence-level label flipping and may produce edits that are fluent but aspect-invalid, semantically drifting, or contradictory. To address this limitation, we propose CAVE-ABSA, a Constraint-Aware Validated Editing framework for generating and validating aspect-level counterfactuals. CAVE-ABSA localizes the opinion span associated with the target aspect, performs controlled counterfactual rewriting, refines candidates through a repair module, and filters them using aspect-level verification, semantic similarity, AMR-guided structural preservation, edit minimality, fluency, and contradiction detection. The framework is designed to construct validated counterfactual ABSA datasets for robustness evaluation and data augmentation. By explicitly separating generation from validation, CAVE-ABSA provides a principled approach for producing meaningful aspect-local counterfactuals and for testing whether ABSA models truly rely on aspect-grounded sentiment reasoning.

</details>


### [121] [Can an Old Dog Be Taught New Tricks? Taking LLMs Beyond Sentence Level Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14040)
*Alaina Brandt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAT系统，结合RAG架构与可比语料库，探索大语言模型超越逐句翻译范式、实现语用层面的整篇文档翻译，并评估其在不同配置下的语篇重组效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动翻译系统（从CAT工具到机器翻译）普遍将翻译视为逐句行为，忽视了语篇组织、修辞风格和语用规范在不同语言间的差异。作者希望推动LLM超越该范式，实现考虑整篇文档语境和可比语料信息的语用翻译。

Method: 提出PAT（Pragmatic Auto-Translator）系统，基于RAG（检索增强生成）架构，将用户配置的翻译规范与来自美式英语和拉美西班牙语可比语料库中的段落级、章节级和文档级示例结合，传递给LLM进行整篇文档生成。评估方面，使用定制的MQM类型学，由两名经过训练的评价者对三组项目的六篇生成式AI主题论文译文进行评估。

Result: 仅使用有限提示时，LLM未产生有意义的语篇重组；加入规范说明和语料库信息后，部分译文显示出显著的重组效果，但并非始终有效。总体而言，LLM可以被引导走向语篇重组、偏离逐句范式，但重组的有效性仍需进一步提升。

Conclusion: LLM有潜力超越逐句翻译范式，实现面向语用的整篇文档翻译，但目前重组的有效性尚不稳定。论文还就自动翻译系统设计、语料库构建以及翻译质量评估方法进行了讨论。

Abstract: Automatic translation systems, from CAT tools to MT, overwhelmingly treat translation as a sentence-by-sentence act. This paper asks whether LLMs can be moved beyond that paradigm through whole-document, corpus-informed translation. We present PAT (Pragmatic Auto-Translator), a RAG-based system that pairs user-configured specifications with context from a comparable corpus of authentic longform texts in U.S. English and Latin American Spanish, passing retrieved paragraph-, section-, and document-level examples to an LLM for whole-document generation. The goal is draft translation for professional verification: target texts reformulated to fit their Spanish-language context, where discourse organization, rhetorical style, and pragmatic norms differ meaningfully from English. We evaluated six automatic translations of essays on generative AI across three projects using a customized MQM typology, assessed by two trained evaluators working from U.S. English into LATAM and Mexican Spanish. Results show that a limited prompt produced no meaningful reformulation, and specifications and corpus-informed translations at times showed substantial reformulation, though not always to effect. We find that LLMs can be moved toward reformulation and away from the sentence-by-sentence paradigm, though more work is needed to improve the effectiveness of those reformulations. In this paper, we discuss considerations related to automatic translation system design, corpus construction, and translation quality evaluation methodology and results.

</details>


### [122] [Hindcast: Replaying Prediction Markets to Evaluate LLM Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14051)
*Xiao Ye,Jacob Dineen,Evan Zhu,Shijie Lu,Kevin Song,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hindcast 通过冻结历史信息快照和设定时间截断点，消除了LLM预测评估中因检索和训练数据时效性导致的信息泄露，从而真正评估模型的预见能力而非回忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于回测的预测评估存在两个关键泄露通道：(1) 检索增强模型可能获取事件发生后的报告，将预测变为查找；(2) 新模型训练数据中包含了过去模型尚未发生的事件。两者都导致评估实际测量的是回忆而非预见。

Method: Hindcast 将模型置于选定的历史时间点 t₀，基于冻结的Reddit公开帖子快照（仅包含 t₀ 之前的内容），对已结算的Polymarket预测市场问题进行评估。每个预测同时与真实结果和 t₀ 时刻的市场价格（人类基于相同历史信息做出的预测）进行对比评分。由于快照固定且截断时间按市场设定，评估可随模型改进反复运行而不过时。

Result: 关闭泄露通道后，检索增强对大多数模型仍有帮助，但仅限于Reddit在事件发生前已有相关讨论的情形；若历史档案中仅包含推测性内容，检索反而不利于预测表现。

Conclusion: Hindcast 提供了一种更严格且可复用的预测能力评估框架，揭示了检索增强在真正的前瞻性预测任务中存在条件依赖性——仅在信息充分时有益，否则可能有害。

Abstract: Forecasters are evaluated by backtesting, which replays resolved questions and grades the probability the system would have assigned before the outcome was known. For LLMs, two channels leak the answer into this test. A model that retrieves can surface reports written after the event, turning forecasting into a lookup, and each new model is trained on data closer to the event, so a question that lay in the future for last year's models sits inside this year's training data. Either way, the test grades recall while claiming to grade foresight. We introduce Hindcast, which closes both leaks by grading a model as if it stood at a chosen past date $t_0$, before the outcome existed in either channel. Hindcast replays resolved Polymarket prediction markets against a frozen snapshot of public Reddit, lets the model read only posts written before $t_0$, and scores each forecast against both what happened and the market's own price at $t_0$, itself a human forecast made from the same past information. Because the cutoff is set per market and the snapshot never changes, the evaluation re-runs on new markets as models improve, without going stale. Once the leak is closed, retrieval still helps most models, but only where Reddit discussed the event beforehand. Where the archive carried only speculation, retrieval hurts.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Anchored Geodesic Analysis for Multivariate Extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13112)
*Alberto Quaini,Chen Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定测地成分分析（AGCA），一种针对正单位球面上极值角律的降维方法。该方法通过约束大子球面必须经过选定的参考方向来近似角变异，默认以平衡完全依赖为锚点。在有限正弦平方测地损失下，总体与经验问题可精确归结为锚定切向离差二阶矩矩阵的特征分析。AGCA 支持尾部模拟，并为组合尾部风险度量提供显式误差界，论文还建立了 top-k 一致性及 oracle 中心极限定理，并在股票组合损失数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端依赖结构在多变量极值理论中由角律刻画，但现有角律降维方法缺乏对参考方向（基准）的约束，导致对近轴和面极值的解释性不足。作者希望提出一种能够以选定基准方向为锚点的降维框架，既保留极值角分布的几何结构，又能为尾部风险度量和尾部模拟提供理论保证。

Method: AGCA 在正单位球面上，通过约束大于球面必须经过锚定方向来近似角变异。默认锚点为平衡完全依赖方向。方法以有限正弦平方测地距离作为损失函数，将总体和经验的降维问题转化为锚定切向离差的二阶矩矩阵特征分解。通过低秩重建，AGCA 得分、载荷、残差风险和解释变异摘要刻画了相对于基准的偏离，并支持尾部模拟。作者还推导了有界 Lipschitz 泛函和齐次尾部得分的显式误差界，建立了 oracle 和经验 AGCA 的 top-k 一致性，以及 oracle 汇总的中心极限定理。

Result: 理论结果表明，AGCA 的 oracle 汇总具有 top-k 一致性，其渐近协方差等于独立同分布样本从极限角律中抽取的协方差。在日度股票组合损失的实证中，AGCA 发现了集中于基准相对尾部方向的结构：10 个成分可解释约 91% 的锚定变异，并且对截尾超额和标准化 VaR 汇总的近似平均相对误差约为 1.25%。

Conclusion: AGCA 为极值角律提供了一种有理论保证的几何降维方法。其锚定机制保证了近轴和面极值情形下的良好定义性，低秩重建支持尾部模拟且误差可控。实证表明该方法在实际金融尾部风险建模中具有简洁且准确的表现，为高维极值依赖结构的降维和模拟提供了新工具。

Abstract: Extremal dependence is naturally described by the angular law of large multivariate observations. We introduce anchored geodesic component analysis (AGCA), a dimension-reduction method for extremal angular laws on the positive unit sphere. AGCA approximates angular variation by great subspheres constrained to pass through a chosen reference direction, with balanced complete dependence as the default anchor. Under a bounded sine-squared geodesic loss, the population and empirical problems reduce exactly to eigenanalysis of a second-moment matrix of anchored tangent departures. The resulting scores, loadings, residual risks and explained-variation summaries describe departures from the benchmark and remain well defined for face and near-axis extremes. Low-rank AGCA reconstructions also support tail simulation: bounded Lipschitz functionals and homogeneous tail scores, including portfolio capped excesses and value-at-risk, inherit explicit error bounds from the AGCA residual risk. We establish top-\(k\) consistency for oracle and rank-Pareto AGCA summaries and an oracle central limit theorem whose covariance is that of an independent sample from the limiting angular law. In daily equity-portfolio losses, AGCA finds concentrated benchmark-relative tail directions: ten components explain about \(91\%\) of anchored variation and approximate capped-excess and normalized value-at-risk summaries with about \(1.25\%\) average relative error.

</details>


### [124] [Deep Simulation-Based Inference for Inhomogeneous Bivariate Log-Gaussian Cox Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13113)
*Qihan Zou,Yan Wang,Tingjin Chu,Zhendong Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种针对非齐次双变量Log-Gaussian Cox过程的两步计算高效估计方法，结合经典泊松估计与神经网络模拟推断，并通过二维图像输入直接学习空间信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维参数估计的复杂性，减少在有协变量时模拟方法需指定宽广参数范围的问题，提升计算效率。

Method: 采用两步法：（1）利用经典泊松估计处理一阶参数；（2）基于神经网络进行模拟推断，估计潜在场参数。引入二维图像输入使模型能直接学习空间信息。

Result: 模拟结果表明该方法能准确估计潜在场参数，并通过大猩猩数据集验证了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该两步估计法在保持估计精度的同时显著降低计算复杂度，对处理包含协变量的非齐次空间点过程具有实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a computationally efficient simulation-based estimation method with a two-step procedure for inhomogeneous bivariate Log-Gaussian Cox Processes. It combines classical Poisson estimation for the first-order parameters with simulation-based inference using neural networks for the latent field parameters. By separating the estimations, it reduces the complexity of high dimensional parameter estimation and the need for the simulation-based method to specify broad parameter ranges in the presence of covariates. In addition, we introduce two dimensional image inputs that enable the model to learn spatial information directly. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach provides accurate estimates of the latent field parameters. We further illustrate the method's practical applicability using the gorilla dataset.

</details>


### [125] [Bandwidth-free nonparametric density estimation for grouped data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13182)
*Furkan Danisman,Hanna Jankowski,Camila P. E. de Souza*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对分组数据（仅知区间内观测频数）的密度估计问题，提出一种均值调整的对数凹（MALC）密度估计方法。该方法无需带宽选择，不依赖特定分布假设，仿真结果表明其在不同分布、样本量和区间宽度下均具有鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际数据采集常因实验不确定性、间歇测量、保密性及未检出等约束，仅能以分组形式（已知区间内频数）获取，而传统密度估计方法多依赖完整数据或特定分布假设。因此需要一种无需带宽、不依赖分布假设的非参数方法来从分组数据中估计底层密度。

Method: 提出均值调整的对数凹（MALC）密度估计方法，针对单变量分组数据，利用对数凹约束进行非参数密度估计，无需带宽选择，也不依赖特定分布假设。

Result: 仿真实验涵盖多种分布、不同样本量和不同区间宽度（grid widths），结果表明MALC方法具有鲁棒性和有效性，相比传统方法适用范围更广。

Conclusion: MALC方法为分组数据分析提供了一种有效的无带宽非参数密度估计方案，适用于更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: In some situations, data is collected under systematical and technical constraints due to uncertainty in experimental reports, intermittent measurements, confidentiality, and non-detects. For this reason, it might not be possible to retrieve or receive the data in a conventional format but rather in a grouped form where only the number of occurrences is known within intervals. The challenge is to estimate the density of the underlying ungrouped data based on the observed grouped data with no information regarding the underlying distribution. To overcome this problem, this study introduces a mean-adjusted log-concave (MALC) density estimation method for univariate grouped data, aiming to provide a bandwidth-free non-parametric approach that does not rely on specific distributional assumptions. The performance of the MALC method is evaluated through simulations across various distributions with different sample sizes and grid widths. The results demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the MALC approach in grouped data analysis, offering a broader range of applications over traditional methods.

</details>


### [126] [MCMC Methods for Parameter Inference in Structurally Nonidentifiable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13243)
*Xuyuan Wang,Donglin Han,Michael Y. Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对结构不可识别常微分方程模型的参数推断问题，提出了两种利用结构可识别性信息的MCMC方法：可识别性感知的几何MCMC和可识别性感知的伪边际MCMC，数值实验表明这两种方法在采样效率和收敛性上均优于标准MCMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构不可识别性在控制理论、系统生物学和公共卫生等领域的ODE模型中普遍存在，导致参数空间中存在观测等价的低维流形，使得标准贝叶斯推断和MCMC方法面临混合差、收敛慢的严重挑战，亟需专门针对非可识别流形结构设计更高效的采样方法。

Method: 提出两种MCMC方法：(1) 可识别性感知的几何MCMC——构建在不可识别流形内部及跨流形移动的提议分布，直接利用流形的几何结构指导采样；(2) 可识别性感知的伪边际MCMC——在可识别参数组合的约化空间上进行推断，再重构完整参数值。两种方法均以正确的后验分布为目标分布。

Result: 理论证明两种方法在标准条件下均具有正确的目标后验分布且满足遍历性。数值算例表明，相比标准MCMC方法，两种方法在采样效率和收敛速度方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将结构可识别性分析的信息融入MCMC算法设计，能够有效克服结构不可识别性带来的推断困难，所提出的两种方法为处理不可识别ODE模型的贝叶斯推断提供了系统且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of parameter inference for ordinary differential equation (ODE) models with structural non-identifiability. Such models arise in a wide range of scientific fields, including control theory, systems biology, and public health. Structural non-identifiability occurs when distinct parameter values provide identical model outputs, resulting in lower-dimensional manifolds of observationally equivalent solutions in the parameter space. This poses challenges for Bayesian inference and Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, often leading to poor mixing and slow convergence. We develop two MCMC methods that use information from structural identifiability analysis. The first, Identifiability-Aware Geometric MCMC, constructs proposals that move within and between non-identifiable manifolds. The second, Identifiability-Aware Pseudo-Marginal MCMC, performs inference on the space of identifiable parameter combinations and reconstructs full parameter values. We show that both methods target the correct posterior distribution and are ergodic under standard conditions. Numerical examples demonstrate improved sampling efficiency and convergence compared with standard MCMC methods.

</details>


### [127] [Average Cause-Specific Hazard: A Censoring-Invariant Measure of Event Burden Under Competing Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13293)
*Khondoker Nazmoon Nabi,Xiang Meng,Lu Tian,Jean M Connors,Deb Schrag,Hajime Uno*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的汇总指标——平均病因别风险率（ACSH），用于在存在竞争事件（尤其是半竞争风险）的情况下，以事件-自由人时为分母，提供不受删失时间分布影响的发病率解释，并开发了相应的非参数估计、推断及两样本比较方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在竞争事件时，传统的累积发生率函数（CIF）和发病率（IR）存在局限：朴素的IR除非强度恒定，否则依赖于删失时间分布，导致其解释和可比性受损。作者旨在提出一种纯粹由事件时间分布定义、不依赖删失时间分布、且保持发病率直观解释的新指标。

Method: 提出ACSH，定义为生存加权的病因别风险率，即对病因别风险函数按无事件生存概率加权平均。开发了基于非参数方法的ACSH估计和推断框架，并针对两样本比较引入ACSH差值和比值，无需对两组间关系做强模型假设即可提供可解释的对比。

Result: 模拟研究验证了该方法在有限样本下的良好性能，并通过CANVAS试验数据的分析展示了所提方法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: ACSH作为一种新的发病率指标，在竞争风险和半竞争风险情境下具有稳健的解释性和良好的统计性质，为临床流行病学研究提供了一种不依赖删失分布的实用工具。

Abstract: Competing events are common in clinical and epidemiologic studies, including semi-competing risks in which a terminal event such as death may follow a nonfatal event but also competes with it beforehand. Standard summaries include the cumulative incidence function (CIF) and the incidence rate (IR), defined as the number of observed events divided by observed event-free person-time. With competing events, the naive IR generally depends on the censoring-time distribution unless intensities are constant. We propose the Average Cause-Specific Hazard (ACSH), a survival-weighted rate per event-free person-time that preserves the interpretation of an incidence rate and is defined purely from the event-time distribution, without involving the censoring-time distribution. We develop nonparametric estimation and inference for ACSH and, for two-sample comparisons, introduce ACSH differences and ratios that provide interpretable contrasts without requiring a strong model assumption between two groups. Simulation studies examine the finite-sample performance, and an analysis of the CANVAS trial illustrates the proposed methods.

</details>


### [128] [Manipulation testing based on Benford's Law for discrete scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13564)
*Roy Cerqueti,Marco Ventura*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出在断点回归设计中，利用本福特定律检测运行变量操纵的新框架。该方法无需研究者设定参数，能定向分解密度行为，识别传统McCrary检验遗漏的系统性操纵，并通过将本福特定律从数字层面推广至概率层面，构建了两个互补检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统McCrary检验存在两大缺陷：一是依赖研究者选择的参数（如箱宽、带宽），可能扭曲结果；二是其对称性设定可能掩盖定向的系统性密度失衡。研究者需要一种无需参数选择、且能精准定位操纵来源的补充性诊断工具。

Method: 提出基于本福特定律的操纵检测框架：（1）开发与本福特定律一致的带宽选择方法；（2）将数据拆分为方向性分量（断点上/下方），分别检验；（3）利用Nigrini (2012)的阈值，将本福特定律从数字分布推广至概率检验层面，构建两个互补检验统计量。

Result: 实证应用表明，该框架能有效识别传统McCrary检验遗漏的系统性操纵，并能精准定位偏差来源（来自断点上方还是下方），为研究者提供了更强的诊断保护价值。

Conclusion: 该框架作为McCrary检验的重要补充和预防性保障，消除了研究者自由参数选择带来的偏差风险，通过方向性分解实现了更精细的操纵检测，值得在断点回归实证研究中推广应用。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of running variable manipulation in Regression Discontinuity Designs. Leveraging the observation that manipulation often alters the density balance around the cutoff, we detect these structural imbalances using Benford's Law -a natural statistical regularity widely applied in fraud detection. Our framework serves as a vital precautionary safeguard alongside traditional McCrary-type tests. It eliminates researcher-chosen parameters that can skew outcomes, while delivering a deeper diagnostic breakdown of the density's behavior. Crucially, whereas the classic McCrary test can overlook systemic imbalances due to its rigid symmetric setup, our method separates the data into directional components. This allows researchers to pinpoint the exact origin of a deviation and spot hidden manipulation that standard frameworks fail to capture. To achieve this, we introduce an innovative method for selecting a bandwidth consistent with BL, and construct two distinct, complementary tests using threshold values adapted from Nigrini (2012) that successfully transition the law's application from digits to probabilities. Empirical applications confirm the enhanced protective value of this diagnostic framework.

</details>


### [129] [Bias Correction for Relative Importance Measures via Doubly Stochastic Reallocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13850)
*Tien-En Chang,Argon Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文分析了相对重要性分析中的两种主流正交化-再分配方法（RW和GCD）的结构性缺陷：RW存在压缩预测变量间差异的"平整化问题"，GCD存在系统性地先验偏向某些预测变量的偏差。作者证明该偏差由再分配矩阵的行和控制，并通过交替投影法和Sinkhorn-Knopp算法将再分配矩阵映射为双随机矩阵，提出GCD-MAP和GCD-SK两种修正方法，其中GCD-SK能有效消除偏差并常优于RW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: General Dominance (GD) 作为相对重要性分析的基准方法，需评估2^p-1个子模型，当预测变量数量p较大时计算量过大。RW和GCD虽计算高效，但各自存在结构性缺陷：RW的"平整化问题"会压缩预测变量间的重要性差异，GCD则存在先验偏差（在观测响应变量之前就系统性地偏向某些预测变量）。目前缺乏对这些缺陷的形式化分析及有效的修正方案。

Method: 作者首先通过再分配矩阵的行和揭示了GCD先验偏差的根源；其次在复合对称假设下进行闭式分析，将GCD和RW的再分配与基于GD的基准相关联，证明纯齐次多重共线性本身不产生先验偏差，并将RW的平整化问题形式化为相对于基准的过度收缩；最后利用交替投影法（MAP）和Sinkhorn-Knopp（SK）算法将GCD的再分配矩阵映射为双随机矩阵，得到修正方法GCD-MAP和GCD-SK，并通过全面模拟验证其性能。

Result: GCD-SK成功消除了结构性的行和偏差，相比原始GCD有显著改进；当第一主成分占主导地位时，GCD-SK通常优于RW。综合模拟结果为不同场景下的方法选择提供了实证指导。

Conclusion: GCD的先验偏差源于再分配矩阵的行和不均匀，通过将其映射为双随机矩阵可有效修正。GCD-SK是推荐的修正方案，在消除偏差的同时保持了计算效率，尤其适用于第一主成分占主导的情形。

Abstract: Relative importance (RI) analysis quantifies each predictor's contribution to the explained variance of a linear model. General Dominance (GD), a widely used benchmark, requires evaluating $2^p-1$ sub-models and becomes computationally intensive as the number of predictors $p$ grows. Orthogonalization-Reallocation Measures (ORMs), including Relative Weights (RW) and the Green--Carroll--DeSarbo measure (GCD), provide efficient alternatives by assigning importance to orthogonalized predictors and reallocating it to the original predictors. Each, however, has a structural limitation: RW exhibits a leveling problem that compresses differences among predictor importance values, whereas GCD exhibits an a priori bias that systematically favors certain predictors before a response is observed. We show that this bias is governed by the row-sums of the reallocation matrix. A closed-form analysis under compound symmetry relates the reallocations underlying GCD and RW to a GD-based benchmark, showing that homogeneous multicollinearity alone does not induce an a priori bias and formalizing RW's leveling problem as excess shrinkage relative to the benchmark. We correct GCD's bias by mapping its reallocation matrix to a doubly stochastic matrix using the Method of Alternating Projections (MAP) and the Sinkhorn--Knopp (SK) algorithm, yielding GCD-MAP and GCD-SK. Comprehensive simulations show that GCD-SK removes the structural row-sum bias, substantially improves upon GCD, and often outperforms RW when the first principal component is dominant. We conclude with empirical guidelines for selecting among the measures.

</details>


### [130] [Verifying formulas for interventional distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13883)
*Francesco Freni,Leonard Henckel,Sebastian Weichwald*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文形式化了因果图模型中的验证问题：判断给定的观测公式是否能识别目标干预分布。该问题与识别问题互补，旨在判断一个已有的公式是否具有识别性。作者提出了一种基于falsifier的实用方法，证明其在正则指数族模型中几乎必然正确，并基于此开发了用于front-door公式中可采纳集合的gateway检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理文献聚焦于识别问题（即是否存在某个公式可以识别目标干预分布），但忽略了验证问题——给定一个具体的公式，它是否确实能识别目标分布？即使拥有完备的识别算法，也无法直接解决验证问题。这一空白在实际应用中至关重要，因为研究者常常需要判断某个候选公式的有效性。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了因果图模型中的验证问题。然后提出falsifier方法：通过搜索反例（即两个在观测分布上一致但在干预分布上不同的模型）来验证公式的正确性。证明在正则指数族模型下，该falsifier诱导出一个几乎必然正确的验证器。最后开发了gateway检验，用于系统性地寻找front-door公式中所有可采纳的变量集合。

Result: 1）证明了完备的识别方法不能直接解决验证问题；2）提出的falsifier在正则指数族模型下几乎必然正确（almost-surely correct）；3）gateway检验能够找出front-door公式中所有可采纳的集合，为实际因果推断提供了可靠工具。

Conclusion: 验证问题是因果推断中一个被忽视但重要的开放问题。本文提出的falsifier方法为验证问题提供了首个实用解决方案，其理论保证和gateway检验展示了该方法的实用性，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We formalize verification in causal graphical models: deciding whether a given observational formula identifies a target interventional distribution. This opens a problem complementary to identification, asking not whether any identifying formula exists, but whether the given formula is identifying. We show that even sound and complete solutions to identification do not solve verification. We propose a falsifier as a first practical route forward, prove that it induces an almost-surely correct verifier for regular exponential-family models, and use the resulting verifier to develop the gateway test, which finds all sets admissible for use in a front-door formula.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [OriginBlame: Record- and Token-Level Data Provenance for AI Training Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13037)
*Haolin Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ob，一个记录级和token级的数据溯源系统，能够将作者身份通过数据处理管线传播，并将撤销请求解析为精确的遗忘集，从而消除数据集级别的过度删除问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，当数据贡献者请求删除其数据时，模型训练者面临实际困难：遗忘算法需要明确的遗忘集，但现有工具无法定位哪些训练记录属于特定作者。现有溯源系统仅工作在文件或数据集级别，导致灾难性的过度删除。

Method: 设计并实现 ob 系统，在记录级和token级追踪数据来源，将作者身份信息通过数据处理管线（如HuggingFace、Datatrove）传播，并通过确定性查询将撤销请求解析为精确的遗忘集。

Result: 在219,555个Wikipedia页面上进行评估：记录级溯源将过度删除从101倍降至1.3倍；在wiki数据上，HuggingFace管线仅增加1.3-4.0%吞吐开销，Datatrove增加2.1-19.0%；在1.7B模型上，基于溯源的遗忘集相比随机基线将遗忘效果提升42%。

Conclusion: 记录级溯源能有效解决数据集级别过度删除问题，以极低的吞吐开销实现精确的遗忘集定位，并显著提升机器遗忘学习的效果。

Abstract: When a data contributor requests removal, model trainers face a practical gap: unlearning algorithms require a forget set, yet no tool can locate which training records belong to a given author. Existing provenance systems operate at file or dataset level, forcing catastrophic over-deletion. We present ob, a record- and token-level data provenance system that propagates author identity through data processing pipelines and resolves revocation requests into precise forget sets via deterministic queries. Evaluation on 219,555 Wikipedia pages demonstrates that record-level provenance eliminates dataset-level over-deletion (from 101x to 1.3x), while integration adds 1.3-4.0% throughput overhead (HuggingFace) and 2.1-19.0% (Datatrove) on wiki data. On a 1.7B model, provenance-based forget sets improve unlearning by 42% over random baselines.

</details>


### [132] [SPINE: Bridging the Cyber-Physical Gap with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13049)
*Minkyu Ham,Dongho Kim,Chan Lee,Jiayi Wang,Min Jun Kim,Yixi Zhang,Guo Ye,Jihai Zhao,Soyeon Park,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPINE是一个基于多智能体的框架，用于系统化调试和部署双臂机器人，使非专业人员也能高效完成机器人校准与遥操作设置，在多个平台上实现了100%的操作成功率，并将部署时间缩短至约14分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽赋予机器人复杂决策的"大脑"，但将智能部署到物理平台仍需繁琐的专家驱动校准，这一"脊髓"层面的部署鸿沟成为可扩展具身智能的主要瓶颈。

Method: SPINE采用双工作流编排：Profile Builder负责构建机器人特定上下文，Debugger通过诊断-修复-验证的循环迭代，直至遥操作成功运行。两者均由多智能体协同驱动。

Result: 在DOBOT X-Trainer的7个调试场景中，SPINE将操作成功率从75%提升至100%，平均遥操作就绪时间从16分45秒降至13分47秒；在AgileX PiPER平台上，SPINE解决了全部10个植入故障，优于专家基线的9/10。

Conclusion: SPINE能够跨双臂平台迁移，降低对专家校准的依赖，推动具身AI向可扩展的实际部署迈进。

Abstract: Foundation models have given robots a sophisticated brain for complex decision-making, yet deploying that intelligence into a physical platform still demands tedious, expert-driven calibration. This deployment gap, the robot's spinal cord, remains a primary bottleneck to scalable Embodied AI. Hence, we propose SPINE (Scalable Physical Integration with ageNtic Expertise): an agentic framework for systematically debugging and deploying bimanual robots with minimal robotics expertise. SPINE's harness comprises two orchestrated multi-agent workflows: a profile builder that creates robot-specific context, and a debugger that cycles through diagnosis, repair, and validation until teleoperation works. Across seven DOBOT X-Trainer debugging scenarios, a robotics novice using SPINE outperformed human operators using Claude Code with the same reference materials, but without SPINE's structured workflow, improving operationalization success from 75% to 100% and reducing mean time-to-teleoperation from 16 min 45 s to 13 min 47 s. On AgileX PiPER, a distinct ROS/CAN bimanual arm, SPINE resolved all 10 implanted bugs, versus 9 out of 10 for the expert baseline, in nearly the same amount of time. Together, these results show that SPINE can transfer across bimanual platforms, reduce dependence on expert calibration, and move embodied AI closer to scalable real-world deployment.

</details>


### [133] [Interventional Grounding Audits: Black-Box Premise-Dependency Tests for LLM Chain-of-Thought via Predicate Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13069)
*Hironao Nakamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出介入式归因审计法，通过替换前提中的谓词为全新符号来检测链式思维推理中各步骤是否真正依赖其声称的前提。在ProntoQA上对GPT-4o进行测试，F1达0.806，远优于自一致性基线（F1=0.343），并发现66%正确解决的问题中存在"答案对但推理错"的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的链式思维推理看似逻辑严密，但可能并未真正依赖其所陈述的前提条件。现有的被动评估方法无法检测这种"表面合理实则脱节"的推理缺陷，因此需要一种能够逐步骤验证前提依赖关系的黑盒审计方法。

Method: 提出介入式归因审计（interventional grounding audits）：对单个前提进行干预，将其目标谓词替换为一个全新的、未出现过的新符号，然后重新运行模型，逐步骤检查每个推理步骤的标准化结论（规范谓词形式）是否发生变化，从而判断该步骤是否真正依赖被干预的前提。在ProntoQA（一个带有金标准证明树的多跳演绎推理基准）上进行评估，分别检测证明树依赖和谓词决定依赖。

Result: 在50个ProntoQA问题上对GPT-4o进行评估：证明树依赖检测F1=0.806，谓词决定依赖检测F1=0.885，召回率达100%，显著优于自一致性基线（F1=0.343，95%自助法置信区间不重叠）。进一步发现66%正确解决的问题中至少存在一个对齐步骤对直接证明树依赖不敏感——全部涉及实体引入前提，揭示了"答案正确但推理错误"的信号。

Conclusion: 介入式归因审计能有效检测链式思维推理中的"未归因"步骤，揭露隐藏的推理缺陷。一致替换评估器存在对实体引入前提的盲点，需结合介入方法加以补充。该方法目前受限于可解析的形式化基准，扩展到非形式化自然语言场景仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models produce chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning that appears logically sound yet may not genuinely depend on its stated premises. We introduce interventional grounding audits, a black-box, step-level test of premise dependency: we intervene on a single premise by substituting its target predicate with a fresh symbol, re-run the model, and check whether each reasoning step's normalized conclusion (canonical predicate form) changes. We evaluate on ProntoQA, a synthetic multi-hop deductive reasoning benchmark with gold proof trees, where step-level premise dependencies are known. Applied to 50 ProntoQA problems with GPT-4o, our method achieves F1 = 0.806 on detecting proof-tree dependencies (F1 = 0.885 on predicate-determining dependencies; Recall = 100%), significantly outperforming a self-consistency baseline (F1 = 0.343; 95% bootstrap CIs non-overlapping). We further identify that 66% of correctly-solved problems contain at least one aligned step insensitive to a direct proof-tree dependency under consistent substitution -- all involving entity-introduction premises, a documented blind spot of the consistent-substitution evaluator -- a "right answer, wrong reasoning" signal invisible to passive methods. All audit certificates, raw outputs, and reproduction scripts are available in a public GitHub repository, and we discuss scope limits beyond formal, parsable benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [Probabilistic Extension of Neuro-Symbolic AGI Robots based on Belnap's Typed Intensional FOL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13073)
*Zoran Majkic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于IFOL_B的神经符号AI系统，通过引入Nilsson概率结构对未知句子进行概率计算，并定义了全局与局部对称变换，利用Shannon最大信息熵原理和神经网络计算概率密度函数KI，以增强系统的认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯神经系统存在可解释性差、缺乏逻辑结构等局限性。作者旨在通过引入概率计算机制和对称变换，扩展IFOL_B框架的认知能力，使其能够基于现有知识库对未知句子进行概率推理，从而构建更强大的概率神经符号通用人工智能。

Method: 1) 基于Nilsson概率结构，对IFOL_B中当前未知的句子进行概率计算；2) 引入全局对称变换（保持当前知识库和逻辑演绎不变）和局部对称变换（用于涉及IFOL_B谓词严格子集的具体子问题的实时决策）；3) 基于Shannon最大信息熵原理，利用神经网络计算两种情形下的概率密度函数KI。

Result: 论文提出了一个完整的理论框架：通过全局对称变换维护知识库一致性，通过局部对称变换支持实时子问题决策，并由神经网络基于最大信息熵原则计算概率密度函数KI，从而赋予神经符号AI系统对未知语句的概率推理能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入概率结构与对称变换机制，成功扩展了IFOL_B框架的认知能力，为构建具备自指能力、逻辑可解释性和概率推理能力的神经符号AGI系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic AI based on $IFOL_B$ is a way to combine neural learning and symbolic reasoning to overcome limitations of purely neural systems (like lack of interpretability and logical structure) with formal logical machinery for self-reference. In this paper we expand the cognitive power of $IFOL_B$ by using the probability computation for the currently unknown sentences, based on Nilsson's probability structure for the $IFOL_B$. We introduce the global symmetry transformation that preserves the current knowledge database and logical deduction, and the local one used for real-time decisions about concrete (sub)problems that involve only a very strict subset of $IFOL_B$ predicates. The computation of probability density function $KI$ in both cases, based on the Shannon's maximum information entropy, is provided by neural networks of this probabilistic neuro-symbolic AGI.

</details>


### [135] [Self-Improvements in Modern Agentic Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13104)
*Zhe Ren,Yimeng Chen,Dandan Guo,Guowei Rong,Tonghui Li,R. B. Xiong,Qingfeng Lan,Wenyi Wang,Li Nanbo,Yibo Yang,Mingchen Zhuge,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统级框架，将现代自改进智能体建模为基础模型与操作脚手架（提示、记忆、工具、控制逻辑）的耦合配置，并将自改进形式化为自诱导更新算子，按更新目标和驱动信号对现有工作进行了系统梳理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进自主智能体正从研究原型走向实际部署，核心目标是实现可控的、无需或仅需极少人工干预的经验驱动进化。然而现有研究分散，缺乏统一的系统级视角来理解和组织各类自改进方法。

Method: 提出一个系统级框架：将智能体定义为"基础模型 + 操作脚手架（提示、记忆、工具、控制逻辑）"的配置耦合；将自改进形式化为自诱导更新算子，该算子获取经验信号并对模型参数或脚手架组件进行更新提交。按更新目标（模型参数 vs. 脚手架组件）和驱动信号（奖励信号、监督信号、自生成信号等）对现有工作进行分类组织。

Result: 通过该框架成功将分散的自改进智能体研究进行了系统化梳理，涵盖了各类更新目标和驱动信号的组合方式，并综述了应用场景和评估方法。

Conclusion: 自改进智能体领域正快速发展但仍面临诸多开放性挑战；本文提供的统一框架有助于研究者系统性地理解该领域，并指出了未来的研究方向。
