Abstract: Longitudinal tumor measurements, dropout information, and genetic covariates provide complementary information about treatment response, but integrating these data sources within a single population modeling framework remains challenging. We extend the empirical Bayes variational autoencoder (EB-VAE) framework to joint longitudinal and time-to-event modeling and evaluate it on tumor growth data. The framework represents inter-individual variability using latent individual effects regularized by a covariate-conditioned empirical Bayes prior, while a decoder maps these latent effects to tumor-volume trajectories. To account for informative dropout, the decoder was augmented with a hazard model, yielding joint predictions of tumor growth and time to dropout. We further compared fully neural and hybrid semi-mechanistic decoder formulations and incorporated genomic covariates through a genetics-conditioned prior adaptation. The hybrid decoder recovered treatment-effect parameters broadly consistent with previously reported nonlinear mixed-effects estimates, while achieving prior predictive performance comparable to the neural decoder. The joint model reproduced both tumor-volume distributions and dropout patterns in held-out individuals, and genetic conditioning improved individual-level prior predictions in both cutaneous melanoma and breast cancer experiments. Stability selection identified several biologically plausible genetic indicators, including alterations in BRAF, NRAS, NF1, and MDM2. These results demonstrate that EB-VAE provides a flexible probabilistic framework for combining neural dynamics, mechanistic structure, time-to-event modeling, and high-dimensional covariates in pharmacometric applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Automatic Differentiation from Scratch: How PyTorch Computes Gradients in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13042)
*Abdeladhim Tahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过具体数值，完整追踪了PyTorch自动微分引擎在物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练中的梯度计算全流程，涵盖物理导数计算和参数梯度反传两个层级，并验证了create_graph=True在图上图（graph-on-graph）机制中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练涉及两层微分——先通过网络计算物理导数$\hat{y}'(t)$，再对该导数依赖的损失函数求参数梯度$\nabla_\theta L$。PyTorch的自动微分引擎如何正确协调这两层微分并不直观，尤其是create_graph=True在图-图微分机制中的作用需要被显式追踪和验证。本文旨在以手算可验证的数值实例，填补从理论理解到工程实现之间的认知鸿沟。

Method: 采用1-3-3-1结构的多层感知机，针对初值问题$y'(t)+y(t)=0, y(0)=1$，逐步追踪三个关键过程：(1) 前向传播中计算图的构建；(2) 反向模式自动微分在单次遍历中计算全部22个参数梯度，每个伴随值均与Tahimi (2026)手工推导的$P/Q$敏感性框架进行对照验证；(3) 通过create_graph=True启用图上图机制，使得物理残差的反传本身保留计算图，从而正确实现二阶微分。

Result: 成功追踪并显式给出每个节点的伴随值，验证了全部22个参数梯度的正确性。确认了create_graph=True使得autograd在计算$\nabla_\theta L$时，将$\hat{y}'(t)$的计算图保留为中间节点，从而通过向量-雅可比积（VJP）正确传播梯度，与Tahimi (2026)的$P/Q$敏感性分析框架完全一致。

Conclusion: PyTorch的自动微分引擎通过图上图机制能够正确、高效地处理PINN所需的双层微分：反向模式autograd在单次遍历中计算所有参数梯度，而create_graph=True确保物理导数的计算图被保留，使得依赖$\hat{y}'(t)$的损失梯度可以继续反传。$P/Q$敏感性框架为理解autograd内部的向量-雅可比积运算提供了严格的理论对应。

Abstract: This paper traces, with explicit numerical values, how PyTorch's automatic differentiation (AD) engine computes gradients for Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) training -- a setting that requires two levels of differentiation: computing the physics derivative $\hat{y}'(t)=d\hat{y}/dt$ through the network, and computing parameter gradients $\nabla_θL$ of a loss that itself depends on $\hat{y}'(t)$. Using a 1-3-3-1 multilayer perceptron and the initial value problem $y'(t)+y(t)=0$, $y(0)=1$, we trace the complete pipeline at every node: the computational graph built during the forward pass, the reverse-mode backward traversal that computes all 22 parameter gradients in a single pass, and the graph-on-graph mechanism by which \texttt{create\_graph=True} enables correct differentiation through the physics-informed residual. Every adjoint value is verified against the hand derivations of Tahimi (2026), connecting the $P/Q$ sensitivity framework to the vector--Jacobian products used by PyTorch's autograd engine.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Backbone Backpropagation: A Decoupled Strategy for Efficient Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13043)
*Daniel Vila-Cruz,Laura Morán-Fernández,Verónica Bolón-Canedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级训练策略，通过适配归一化层并解耦特征提取与分类器优化，仅预计算一次特征，配合基于间隔的加权损失重设计分类头，在多种CNN/Transformer架构和医学数据集上大幅降低训练时间与碳排放，同时保持或超越基线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像分类模型虽精度高，但部署面临计算成本高和能耗大的挑战，尤其在资源受限的临床或原型开发环境中，需要一种高效且环保的训练方案。

Method: 提出轻量级训练策略：(1) 适配模型的归一化层至新领域；(2) 将特征提取与分类器优化解耦，仅预计算一次特征以降低开销；(3) 重新设计分类器头部，引入基于间隔的加权损失（margin-based weighted loss），无需端到端反向传播即可减少类别模糊性。

Result: 在四种CNN架构（ResNet18、ResNet50、MobileNet、DenseNet121）、三种Transformer模型（ViT、Swin、DeiT）和三个医学数据集（Brain Cancer MRI、BreakHis、PatchCamelyon）上评估，训练时间显著减少，精度仅轻微下降，且常匹配或超越基线性能，CO₂排放降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法在几乎不牺牲精度的前提下大幅提升训练效率，显著降低碳排放，为资源受限的临床及原型开发环境提供了一种实用且环境可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art image classification but face deployment challenges due to computational costs and energy demands. We propose a lightweight training strategy that adapts normalization layers of the model to the new domain and decouples feature extraction from classifier optimization, reducing overhead by precomputing features only once. A redesigned classifier head with margin-based weighted loss further minimizes ambiguity without end-to-end backpropagation. Evaluated across four CNN architectures (ResNet18, ResNet50, MobileNet, DenseNet121), three Transformer models (ViT, Swin and DeiT) and three medical datasets (Brain Cancer MRI, BreakHis and PatchCamelyon), our approach significantly reduces the required training time with only a marginal accuracy trade-off, often matching or surpassing baseline performance. This efficiency translates to reducing CO2 by orders of magnitude, offering a practical and environmentally sustainable solution for resource-constrained clinical or prototyping environments.

</details>


### [172] [Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence: Roles, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13045)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦可解释人工智能（FedXAI）这一新兴范式，阐述可解释性如何从后置工具演变为联邦学习生命周期中的核心组件，并提出分类法对方法进行梳理，同时指出标准化评估、非IID数据下的解释、安全性等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习保护了数据隐私，但模型仍是不透明的“黑箱”；可解释AI提升了透明度与信任，但两者结合面临隐私与可解释性的双重需求。FedXAI旨在同时满足这两类需求，尤其在高风险领域中至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的梳理与统一框架。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法，提出一个多维度分类法，按可解释性的角色、模型与解释器类型、解释范围、集成层级、联邦学习设置及数据异构性对FedXAI方法进行分类；覆盖了从模型无关解释到可解释联邦模型，以及解释感知聚合机制等方法，并审视了评估实践。

Result: 综述揭示了可解释性已从后验工具发展为支撑聚合、个性化、鲁棒性、协调及系统决策的关键组件；发现当前缺乏标准化基准与指标来评估解释质量、稳定性、隐私泄露和计算开销；识别出非IID数据下的可解释性、解释中心安全威胁、通信高效XAI、持续FedXAI及领域知识集成等核心挑战。

Conclusion: FedXAI是构建可信、透明且隐私保护的联邦AI系统的关键方向。本文为研究者提供了结构化参考框架，并明确了未来需要重点突破的研究空白，包括标准化评估体系、非IID鲁棒解释、安全性防御及与监管框架的对齐。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a key paradigm for privacy-preserving collaborative model training across distributed and heterogeneous data sources. By keeping raw data local, FL addresses data confidentiality concerns, yet it does not resolve the opacity of modern machine learning models. In parallel, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has gained attention for improving transparency, trust, and accountability, particularly in high-stakes domains. Their intersection has given rise to Federated Explainable Artificial Intelligence (FedXAI) paradigm, which aims to jointly satisfy privacy and explainability requirements. This survey provides a systematic review of FedXAI, highlighting the transition of explainability from a post-hoc tool to an integral component of the FL lifecycle. We show how explainability supports aggregation, personalization, robustness, coordination, and system-level decision making. To organize the literature, we introduce a taxonomy that classifies FedXAI methods by the role of explainability, model and explainer types, explanation scope, integration level, FL settings, and data heterogeneity. We review approaches ranging from model-agnostic explanations to interpretable federated models and explainability-aware aggregation mechanisms. We also examine evaluation practices and discuss the lack of standardized benchmarks and metrics for measuring explanation quality, stability, privacy leakage, and computational overhead. Finally, we identify key challenges, including explainability under non-IID data, explanation-centric security threats, communication-efficient XAI, continual FedXAI, and the integration of domain knowledge and regulatory constraints. By consolidating existing work and identifying key gaps, this survey serves as a reference framework for designing trustworthy, transparent, and privacy-preserving federated AI systems.

</details>


### [173] [What Your Model Threw Away and Why You'll Want It Back: Masking, Fingerprinting, and Privacy from Discarded Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13046)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个框架，用于分析机器学习模型在具有李群作用的输入空间中所丢弃的对称性信息。作者定义了"零纤维"（null fiber）和"稳定化子"（Stab）两个对象，用以刻画模型无法感知的对称变换，并提供了基于牛顿迭代的高效计算方法，最终在分子性质预测和球面图像分类等任务上验证了该框架在数据掩蔽、模型指纹识别和隐私保护计算中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型常利用数据中的对称性（如等变性或不变性）来提升泛化能力，但模型究竟"丢弃"了哪些对称性信息、这些被丢弃的信息如何被量化和利用，尚缺乏系统的理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为理解模型的对称性盲区提供严格的数学工具。

Method: 1. **数学定义**：给定李群表示 π: G → GL(V) 和光滑函数 f: V → ℝ，定义逐点零纤维 N_G(f,x) = {g ∈ G : f(π(g⁻¹)·x) = f(x)} 和全局稳定化子 Stab_G(f)。2. **维数下界**：利用原像定理证明，对于光滑实值函数，零纤维在一般点处维数至少为 dim G - 1，与模型架构无关。3. **紧群谱表征**：在群作用于自身的设定下，借助 Peter-Weyl 定理，用傅里叶系数矩阵给出零纤维和稳定化子的谱刻画。4. **数值计算**：通过对轨道映射施加牛顿迭代，以少量梯度评估的代价高效计算零纤维元素。

Result: 理论结果表明零纤维的维数下界具有架构无关的普适性；数值实验在 SO(3) 分子性质预测和 PSL(2,ℂ)（Möbius 群）球面图像分类两个任务上验证了框架的有效性，展示了其在数据掩蔽、模型指纹识别和隐私保护计算中的应用潜力。该框架统一适用于经典神经网络和变分量子电路。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解和利用机器学习模型丢弃的李群对称性信息提供了严格的数学基础，其架构无关性保证了广泛适用性。通过高效数值算法，可将模型对特定对称变换的"盲区"转化为数据隐私保护、模型溯源等实际应用，为对称性感知学习理论提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a framework for the information discarded by machine learning models whose inputs carry a Lie group action. Given a representation $π$ of a Lie group $G$ on a space $V$ and a learned function $f\colon V \to \mathbb{R}$, we define two objects measuring the symmetry invisible to $f$. The null fiber at a point $x \in V$ is the set $N_G(f,x) = \{g \in G : f(π(g^{-1}) \cdot x) = f(x)\}$ of group elements whose inverse action on $x$ is undetectable by $f$. When $N_G(f,x)$ is independent of $x$, it coincides with the stabilizer $\mathrm{Stab}_G(f)$, the largest subgroup of $G$ under which $f$ is invariant. For smooth maps to $\mathbb{R}$, the preimage theorem guarantees that null fibers have dimension at least $\dim G - 1$ at generic inputs, regardless of architecture. For compact groups acting on themselves, the Peter--Weyl theorem yields a spectral characterization of both objects in terms of the Fourier coefficient matrices of $f$. We show that null fiber elements can be computed efficiently via Newton iteration on the orbit map, at a cost comparable to a few gradient evaluations. Applications to data masking, model fingerprinting, and privacy-preserving computation are developed and tested experimentally on molecular property prediction under $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ and spherical image classification under the Möbius group $\mathrm{PSL}(2, \mathbb{C})$. The framework applies uniformly to classical neural networks and variational quantum circuits.

</details>


### [174] [Targeted Recovery of Weight-Space Mechanisms From Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13047)
*Antoine Vigouroux,Lee Sharkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出目标参数分解(tPD)，通过引入高秩"全能"组件处理非目标数据，仅识别处理特定输入的计算组件，大幅降低大规模模型的分解成本，并在Transformer语言模型上验证了其有效性和机械忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数分解(PD)虽能将神经网络分解为可解释的计算组件，但扩展到大型模型时计算成本极高、风险大。作者旨在解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，使PD能以较低成本应用于大规模模型。

Method: 提出目标参数分解(tPD)：引入一个高秩的"全能"(catch-all)组件处理所有非目标数据，仅对处理特定目标输入(从孤立提示到大子任务)的组件进行识别和分解，从而避免对整个网络进行全面分解。

Result: 在玩具模型和基于The Pile训练的Transformer语言模型上验证了tPD。在4层Transformer上仅用已发表分解方法7%的FLOPs提取了CSS-only子模型；在12层Transformer上对记忆序列进行精确消融和重连，对其它输入的副作用可忽略不计。

Conclusion: tPD能够以极小计算代价恢复可复现、机械忠实的电路，为大规模模型的可解释性研究提供了可扩展的实用工具。

Abstract: Parameter decomposition (PD) decomposes neural networks into interpretable computational components that faithfully reflect the original network's operations. However, scaling PD to large models requires vast compute, making it a costly and risky endeavor. Here we propose targeted PD (tPD), which identifies only the components that process specific inputs of interest -- from isolated prompts to large subtasks -- by introducing a high-rank catch-all component that handles all non-target data. We validate tPD on toy models and on transformer language models trained on The Pile, where it recovers reproducible, mechanistically faithful circuits. We extract a CSS-only submodel of a 4-block transformer using 7% of the FLOPs of its published decomposition, and in a 12-block transformer we surgically ablate and rewire memorized sequences, with negligible side effects on other inputs.

</details>


### [175] [Uncertainty-Aware Sequential Decision Rules for Event-Triggered LLM Invocation in Streaming Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13048)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将流式推理中何时调用LLM的问题形式化为基于风险的序贯停止问题，提出了一个统一的理论框架，证明了触发策略的六项理论性质（包括最小事件间隔、阈值策略最优性、近似SPRT保证、次线性遗憾界等），并在涡轮风扇退化数据上通过真实LLM调用进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式推理管道越来越多地将轻量级快速模型与LLM配对使用，LLM提供丰富的语义理解但代价高昂。然而，"何时调用LLM"这一核心问题缺乏正式的理论处理。现有触发策略（事件触发、SPRT、CUSUM等）分散且缺乏统一分析框架。

Method: 将LLM调用决策建模为基于风险的序贯停止问题：当观测历史上的风险泛函超过阈值时触发策略激活。在该框架下证明六项理论结果，涵盖：抑制抖振的最小事件间隔界、smooth-pasting阈值最优性、参数估计下的近似SPRT保证、平稳流的O(√(T log T))遗憾界（扩展至C_T变点下的O(√((C_T+1)T log T))）、在线梯度下降的自适应阈值收敛O(1/√T)、以及校准-漏检率转移不等式。在CMAPSS涡轮风扇退化数据上使用真实LLM调用进行实证验证。

Result: 理论方面，证明了阈值策略的最优性及多项收敛保证，多个经典触发族可表达为该框架的特例。实证方面：所有原则性触发器的遗憾指数α<1，验证了次线性遗憾；在1600次LLM诊断中，92.9%的grounding score≥0.75；异常分数驱动的风险函数在Pareto AUC上以约一个数量级的优势超越替代方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险基序贯停止框架为流式推理中LLM调用时机提供了严格的理论基础，统一了多种经典触发策略。理论和实验一致表明，该方法在保持高诊断质量的同时实现了次线性遗憾，异常分数驱动的风险函数表现显著优于其他设计。

Abstract: Streaming inference pipelines increasingly pair lightweight fast models with Large Language Models (LLMs) that provide rich semantic understanding at substantial cost. The central question of when to invoke the LLM has received limited formal treatment. We cast this as a risk-based sequential stopping problem, where a trigger policy fires when a risk functional over the observation history exceeds a threshold. Within this framework, we prove six results: a minimum inter-event time bound excluding trigger chattering; optimality of threshold policies via smooth pasting; approximate SPRT guarantees under estimated parameters; O(sqrt(T log T)) regret for stationary streams, extending to O(sqrt((C_T + 1) T log T)) under C_T changepoints; O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence of online gradient descent for adaptive thresholds; and a calibration-to-miss-rate transfer inequality. Several classical trigger families, including event-triggered, optimal stopping, SPRT, CUSUM, and Bayesian triggers, can be expressed as special cases of this framework. On turbofan degradation data (CMAPSS) with real LLM calls, we empirically verify the theoretical assumptions, ablate the risk function design, compare against six baselines including a RouteLLM-style router and contextual bandits, and analyze cost sensitivity and LLM failure modes. The results confirm sublinear regret, with alpha < 1 for all principled triggers; high diagnostic quality, with 92.9 percent of 1600 LLM diagnoses reaching grounding score >= 0.75 under our rubric; and that anomaly-score-driven risk functions dominate alternatives by roughly an order of magnitude on the Pareto AUC.

</details>


### [176] [TSSM: Triaxial State Space Model for Global Station Weather Forecasting with Temporal-Variable-Historical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13101)
*Songru Yang,Zili Liu,Tao Han,Ben Fei,Fenghua Ling,Lei Bai,Chang Liu,Xiangyang Ji,Zhenwei Shi,Zhengxia Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSSM提出一种三轴状态空间模型，通过引入历史增强的时间-变量-历史三轴扫描范式，将周期对齐的历史气象数据纳入预测，弥补传统方法仅依赖短期回看窗口的不足，在Weather-5K数据集上实现SOTA性能，并在极端事件预测、长时程预报和缺失观测鲁棒性方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全球台站天气预报方法过度依赖短期时序模式（look-back window），难以捕捉混沌气象动力学中的长周期、大尺度规律，导致极端事件预测能力差、误差累积严重，尤其在部分观测条件下更为明显。需要一种能有效融合长期历史信息的新型建模范式。

Method: 提出三轴状态空间模型（TSSM），核心包括：（1）将历史样本按周期对齐堆叠成批次，使预测同时获得历史和当前观测的因果支持；（2）设计时间扫描、变量扫描和历史扫描三条轴，分别捕获轴向时序依赖、变量间相关性及历史演变规律；（3）采用层次化共享结构，统一建模从季节性到极端事件的多尺度模式，缓解历史模式间的错位问题。

Result: 在最大台站气象数据集Weather-5K上，TSSM达到SOTA：精度提升10%，极端事件指标提升61%；在人工标注数据集上95%的指标获得最优或次优。长时程（240h）预报增益达37.5%，48h×5迭代设置下增益高达103.5%。在高达80%观测缺失条件下，TSSM仍保持90%以上性能，而基线方法降至43%以下。

Conclusion: TSSM通过三轴扫描与历史增强范式，有效克服了传统方法对短期窗口的依赖，显著提升了台站天气预报的精度、极端事件捕捉能力、长时程预报稳定性和缺失数据鲁棒性，为全球原位观测网络中可靠的气象预测提供了实用化方案。

Abstract: Global Station Weather Forecasting (GSWF) is pivotal for localized and extreme weather prediction over key regions. Despite efforts to exploit look-back windows, existing methods show limited accuracy gains and struggle with extreme events and error accumulation. These limitations stem from overreliance on short-term patterns, which are insufficient to capture chaotic weather dynamics, especially under partial observations. To address this problem, we propose a novel Triaxial State Space Model (TSSM) with a history-enhanced Temporal-VariableHistorical paradigm, which incorporates period-aligned historical weather data to compensate for long-term, large-scale periodic, and full-window weather patterns beyond the temporal lookback window. Specifically, TSSM stacks historical samples into period-aligned batches, where forecasting is causally supported by historical and current observations. Temporal, variable, and historical scanning are designed to capture axial temporal dependencies, variable correlations, and historical evolution. This structure is hierarchically shared to model seasonal to extreme events while alleviating misalignment across historical patterns. TSSM achieves SOTA performance on Weather-5K, the largest station weather dataset to date, with 10% and 61% gains in accuracy and extreme event metrics, and obtains 95% best or second-best results on human-involved datasets. Its advantages are more pronounced in long-horizon and iterative forecasting, reaching a 37.5% gain at 240h and up to 103.5% under a 48h times 5 iterative setting. Moreover, TSSM retains > 90% performance under up to 80% missing observations, compared with < 43% for baselines, demonstrating robustness and practical potential for reliable GSWF in global in-situ observation networks.

</details>


### [177] [Gauge-Invariant, Parameter-Insensitive Regularization for Potential Recovery from Flow on Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13609)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在有向图上从观测流恢复潜在势是一个不适定问题。标准做法——岭回归——会引入规范(norm)相关的人为偏差，导致排序崩溃甚至反转。作者采用规范不变的图 Dirichlet 能量替代岭惩罚，解决了该问题，使估计对正则化参数 λ 在四个数量级范围内不敏感，并保留动态范围。该思想进一步推广到图神经网络中，可防止深层有向 GCN 的过度平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有向图的离散 Poisson 问题中，从流恢复节点势是广泛存在的逆问题，但该问题因常数模式的零空间而不适定。常规的岭正则化虽能消除零空间，却引入了一个依赖于所选规范的、无物理意义的原点，导致势的排序和动态范围在正则化下崩溃甚至反转。作者旨在揭示这一被忽视的规范依赖性危害，并提供一种规范不变的解决方案。

Method: 作者诊断出岭回归的失败源于其对零空间施加的 ℓ² 规范惩罚，该惩罚将解拉向无意义的原点。解决方案是采用规范不变的图 Dirichlet 能量（即 H¹ 半范数）作为正则化项。具体做法是：将问题限制在均值零空间（商掉常数模式），构造一个对称正定(SPD)的约化系统求解。通过 Poisson 残差来定位吸收边界。此外，将该规范不变原理应用于图神经网络中——逐层中和常数模式，以防止有向 GCN 中的过度平滑。

Result: 在合成数据上，规范不变估计在 λ 跨越四个数量级时保持排序稳定，而岭回归对任意 λ>0 都会反转排序（秩相关从 +0.81 跌至 -0.42）。在三个公开点击流数据集上，规范不变估计保留了 28%-41% 的内部动态范围，而岭回归仅保留 0.2%。消融实验表明结果对提取方法鲁棒。推广至图神经网络中，逐层移除常数模式有效防止了深层有向 GCN 的过度平滑。

Conclusion: 规范不变性是有向图上逆问题的关键：用图 Dirichlet 能量替代岭正则化，既可获得参数不敏感性，又能完整保留动态范围。该思想不仅解决了经典的流-势恢复问题，还通过链接到图神经网络中的过度平滑问题，为图学习领域提供了统一的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Recovering a latent potential from observed flow on a directed graph (a discrete Poisson problem with Dirichlet boundaries) is ill-posed, and the standard fix backfires: ridge regularization shrinks toward a gauge-meaningless origin, collapsing and reversing the recovered ordering ($+0.81\to-0.42$ rank correlation against a planted ground truth). The gauge-invariant graph Dirichlet energy removes the hazard and delivers parameter-insensitivity: the estimate is stable across four orders of magnitude in $λ$, whereas ridge inverts the ordering for every $λ>0$. We prove the reduced solve is SPD and preserves dynamic range exactly where ridge collapses it, and localize absorbing boundaries from flow alone via a Poisson residual. The $H^1$ seminorm is classical; what is new is the gauge diagnosis, the parameter-insensitivity it buys, and an ablation showing the result is robust to the extraction method. On three public clickstream corpora the gauge-invariant estimate retains $28$--$41\%$ of the interior dynamic range while ridge collapses to as little as $0.2\%$. The same gauge invariance carries into graph neural networks -- neutralizing the constant mode per layer prevents the oversmoothing that collapses a deep directed GCN -- linking this classical inverse problem to a central question in graph learning.

</details>


### [178] [Disentangling Knowledge States with Ability and Proficiency Modeling for Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13103)
*Duantengchuan Li,Yingqian Bi,Jinsong Chen,Rui Zhang,Mingwen Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAKT 将学生交互序列分解为能力构建阶段和熟练应用阶段，使用多分支 Transformer 和类型感知读出模块分别建模阶段特定与整体知识状态，并通过因果分析揭示阶段混淆带来的混杂偏差，在六个数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪方法将原始交互序列视为统一行为过程，忽视了学习行为的阶段性特征。作者初步观察到学生在充分练习后更可能正确回答此前错误的知识概念，表明学习存在从能力构建到熟练应用的过渡，因此需要分阶段建模以捕捉不同学习阶段的独特模式。

Method: 提出 Phase-Aware Knowledge Tracing (PAKT) 框架：(1) 设计定制分解机制，根据学生历史表现将交互序列分解为能力构建阶段和熟练应用阶段；(2) 构建多分支 Transformer 架构，分别编码两个阶段序列及整体序列；(3) 设计类型感知读出模块，融合阶段特定和整体知识状态进行最终预测；(4) 通过因果分析揭示阶段无关 KT 模型中因纠缠复杂学习行为而产生的混杂偏差。

Result: 在六个公开基准数据集上进行广泛实验，PAKT 一致优于代表性基线方法，AUC 最大提升 1.33%，平均提升 0.82%。

Conclusion: 将学习过程分解为能力与熟练两个阶段能有效提升知识追踪性能，分阶段建模有助于更精准地刻画学生知识状态的动态演化，为知识追踪领域提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) aims to predict students' future performance by modeling their evolving knowledge states from historical interactions. Existing KT methods usually treat the raw interaction sequence as a unified behavioral process, overlooking the phase-specific nature of learning behaviors. Our preliminary observations show that students are more likely to correctly answer previously failed knowledge concepts after sufficient practice, suggesting a transition from ability-building to proficiency-oriented learning. Motivated by this, we propose Phase-Aware Knowledge Tracing (PAKT), a KT framework that decomposes student interactions into ability and proficiency phases based on the tailored decomposition mechanism. To effectively exploit the decomposed sequences, we design a multi-branch Transformer with a type-aware readout module to jointly capture phase-specific and holistic knowledge states. We further provide a causal analysis to reveal the confounding bias caused by entangling complex learning behaviors in phase-agnostic KT models. Extensive experiments on six public benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms representative baselines, with a maximum AUC gain of 1.33% and an average gain of 0.82%.

</details>


### [179] [DAGR: State-Conditioned Goal Representations via Difference-Aware Goal Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13731)
*Xing Lei,Wenyan Yang,Xuetao Zhang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAGR 通过多尺度门控交叉注意力将现有静态目标编码器细化为状态条件嵌入，在导航任务上取得提升，但该提升主要源于门控残差机制而非核心命名的差异偏置，且在操作和拼图任务上表现持平或下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件强化学习的编码器（对比、度量、时序距离、信息论等）均为状态无关的静态嵌入，无法标识目标的哪一部分仍需执行动作。策略被迫通过逆向推断两个编码器来恢复这一线索，增加了学习难度。

Method: 提出 DAGR（Difference-Aware Goal Cross-Attention），将任意后融合编码器的静态嵌入细化为状态条件嵌入。核心包含两个组件：(1) 近恒等门控残差连接，保护基础表示不被破坏；(2) 基于 token 级状态-目标差异图的差异感知交叉注意力，用于偏置注意力分数。整体采用多尺度门控交叉注意力架构。

Result: 在 OGBench 导航基准上 DAGR 取得性能提升。消融实验表明，增益归因于门控残差机制，而非方法命名的差异偏置。在操作和拼图任务上，DAGR 与基线持平或略低。

Conclusion: DAGR 是一种结构化细化方法而非通用改进方案，其有效性具有任务依赖性，门控残差而非差异感知才是性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning hinges on how the goal is encoded. Contrastive, metric, temporal-distance, and information-theoretic encoders differ in objective. They still share one trait. None of them sees the current state. Such a state-independent embedding cannot mark which part of the goal still needs action. The policy must then recover that cue by inverting both encoders. We propose DAGR. It refines the static embedding of any late-fusion encoder into a state-conditioned one through multi-scale gated cross-attention. A near-identity gated residual preserves the base representation. Difference-aware Goal Cross-Attention then biases the attention scores using a per-token state-goal discrepancy map. On OGBench, DAGR improves navigation. Our ablations trace the gain to the gated residual, not to the difference bias that names the method. On manipulation and puzzle tasks it matches or falls below the base. DAGR is a structured refinement, not a universal improvement.

</details>


### [180] [STKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13108)
*Sicong Lai,Yuehong Hu,Siru Zhong,Si Qiao,Yuxuan Liang,Guangyin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STKAN，一种将Taylor多项式KAN模块引入时空混合的交通预测架构，通过可学习软节点分组、分组空间混合、时间依赖建模及自注意力机制，在五个基准上取得竞争力性能，表明非线性函数逼近器的设计是时空预测架构设计的有益补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测研究过度聚焦于图结构、注意力机制和分解架构的创新，而忽视了底层非线性函数逼近器的影响。真实交通数据具有异质性空间相关性和非线性时间动态，需要重新审视函数逼近器的作用。

Method: 提出STKAN架构：（1）采用可学习的软节点分组分配机制构建高层空间表示；（2）在分组层面进行空间混合；（3）在压缩序列上建模时间依赖；（4）引入空间和时间自注意力层捕获长程交互；（5）核心创新在于将Taylor多项式KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold Network）模块用于空间和时间token混合，替代传统MLP。

Result: 在五个交通预测基准数据集上的实验表明，STKAN达到有竞争力的性能，且在测试设置中优于基于MLP的变体。

Conclusion: 非线性函数逼近器的设计可作为时空预测中架构设计的有用补充，KAN模块在时空混合中展现了良好潜力。

Abstract: Real-world traffic data exhibit heterogeneous spatial correlations and nonlinear temporal dynamics, posing substantial challenges for accurate spatio-temporal forecasting. Existing approaches have developed increasingly sophisticated graph, attention, and decomposition architectures, while the influence of the underlying nonlinear function approximator has received comparatively less attention. In this work, we propose STKAN, a spatio-temporal forecasting architecture that introduces Taylor-polynomial Kolmogorov--Arnold Network modules into spatial and temporal token mixing. STKAN first constructs high-level spatial representations through a learnable soft node-group assignment mechanism, applies group-wise spatial mixing, and subsequently models temporal dependencies over the compressed sequence. Spatial and temporal self-attention layers are further employed to capture long-range interactions. Experiments on five traffic forecasting benchmarks show that STKAN achieves competitive performance and performs better than the evaluated MLP-based variant in the tested settings. These results suggest that the design of nonlinear function approximators can serve as a useful complement to architectural design in spatio-temporal forecasting.

</details>


### [181] [Algebraic Representability as the Limiting Regime of Grokking: An Exactly Solvable Model with Holomorphic Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13749)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Xavier Cadet,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了使用单项式激活函数的两层网络在模算术任务上的表现，发现当可表达函数类坍缩为有限维代数簇时，网络呈现出二元的"瞬间成功或彻底失败"行为，不存在grokking现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络在模算术任务中grokking现象的极端情况：当模型架构的可表达函数类坍缩为有限维代数簇时，网络行为会发生什么变化？

Method: 采用两层网络，使用全纯单项式激活函数σ(z)=z^k，在通过单位根编码的模算术任务上训练。通过代数方法完整刻画输出子空间，分析离散傅里叶支撑条件，并在585次运行中验证理论预测。

Result: 网络输出被限制在(Z_p)^2的特征的(k+1)维子空间内；任务可表示的充要条件是其离散傅里叶支撑位于对角线u+v≡k (mod p)上；非可表示目标即使在训练集上也无法拟合，训练损失存在正下界；585次运行中代数预测与实验结果吻合度达99.8%，无记忆化阶段和grokking现象。

Conclusion: 这种二元行为是容量-grokking关系的极限情形：当可表达类收缩为固定代数对象时，网络能否grok的问题退化为能否表示目标的问题。瓶颈消融实验将这一极端与标准网络连接起来，描绘了从表示失败到记忆化无泛化再到grokking的连续路径。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on modular arithmetic exhibit grokking, a delayed transition from memorisation to generalisation known to depend on model capacity: too little and the network memorises slowly or not at all, too much and it generalises almost immediately. What happens at the extreme of this spectrum, when the architecture's expressible function class collapses to a finite-dimensional algebraic variety? We study two-layer networks with a holomorphic monomial activation sigma(z)=z^k, trained on modular tasks encoded via roots of unity. Here the network output, regardless of hidden width, is confined to a (k+1)-dimensional subspace of characters of (Z_p)^2, an O(k/p^2) slice of the full function space. We give a complete algebraic characterisation of this subspace: a task is representable if and only if its discrete Fourier support lies on the diagonal u+v = k (mod p), which for linear-phase targets reduces to the arithmetic criterion m+n=k. This is not merely a constraint on eventual generalisation but on memorisation itself: because the outputs are algebraically confined, a non-representable target cannot be fit even on the training set, and we prove a positive lower bound on the training loss, independent of width. Across 585 runs the algebraic prediction matches the observed outcome with 99.8% accuracy, with no memorisation regime and no grokking; outcomes split cleanly into instant success and outright failure. This binary behaviour is the limiting case of the capacity-grokking relationship: when the expressible class shrinks to a fixed algebraic object, the question of when a network will grok dissolves into whether it can represent the target at all. A bottleneck ablation connects this extreme to standard networks, tracing a continuous path from representational failure, through memorisation without generalisation, to grokking with a shrinking gap as capacity grows.

</details>


### [182] [A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13110)
*Yi Wang,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Samba，一种基于混合Mamba架构的音视频导航模型，通过Mamba状态编码器替代传统GRU进行时序聚合，并构建音频Mamba编码器捕捉频谱图全局时频依赖，在Matterport3D和Replica数据集上显著超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2020年以来，以CNN和循环架构为核心的音视频导航骨干网络已五年未发生本质变化，难以有效支撑动态多模态序列的高效表征，亟需架构层面的革新。

Method: 提出Samba模型，核心包含两大组件：(1) 自适应选择机制的Mamba状态编码器（M-SE），替代传统GRU进行时序状态聚合；(2) 音频Mamba编码器（AME），弥补卷积算子在捕捉频谱图全局时频依赖关系上的不足。

Result: Samba在面对未听过的声源和未见过的场景时展现出优异的泛化能力。在Matterport3D数据集上，导航成功率（SR）相比现有SOTA提升11.3%；在场景结构更精细的Replica数据集上，性能增益更为显著。

Conclusion: 以Mamba为核心的架构重构，在更低计算成本下释放了更强的具身表征能力，为音视频导航领域的范式演进提供了一条鲁棒的技术路径。

Abstract: Since the paradigm centered on convolutional neural networks and recurrent architectures was established in 2020, the fundamental backbone networks for audio-visual navigation have undergone no essential changes for more than five years, making them inadequate to support efficient representation of dynamic multimodal sequences. This paper proposes Samba(A Hybrid Mamba for Audio-Visual Navigation). It uses the adaptive selection-enabled Mamba State Encoder (M-SE) to replace conventional GRUs for temporal aggregation, and constructs an Audio Mamba Encoder (AME) to remedy the limitations of convolutional operators in capturing global time-frequency dependencies in spectrograms. Experiments demonstrate that Samba exhibits exceptional generalization performance when facing unheard sound sources and unseen scenes. On the Matterport3D dataset, it improves the navigation success rate (SR) by 11.3\% compared with existing state-of-the-art models, and the performance gain is even more pronounced on the Replica dataset, which features finer scene structures. Such modernized architectural reconstruction unlocks stronger embodied representation capabilities at a lower computational cost, thereby providing a highly robust technical pathway for paradigm evolution in the field of audio-visual navigation.

</details>


### [183] [Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching via Random Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13841)
*Zhouhao Yang,Yezhen Wang,Kenji Kawaguchi,Vladimir Braverman,Haoyang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HTFM 将重尾分布建模为以随机时钟为条件的高斯尺度混合，通过对时钟路径进行对数符号特征编码，使流匹配模型能适配重尾数据，在保持低NFE采样优势的同时提升模式覆盖与尾部统计恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散与流匹配模型以高斯噪声为源分布，虽训练目标易处理，但与重尾数据的归纳匹配性差，难以捕获稀有事件的尾部特征。

Method: 提出 Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching via Random Clocks (HTFM)：将重尾源分布视为时钟条件高斯源的混合；条件于某时钟路径时，源分布与流为高斯形式，边缘化后得到涵盖高斯、α-稳定和 Student-t 族的高斯尺度混合；利用截断对数符号特征编码路径值时钟，使速度场能以极小开销适应条件空间。

Result: 在二维不平衡 α-稳定混合分布、CIFAR10-LT 及 HRRR 天气场数据上，HTFM 在模式覆盖、样本质量和尾部统计恢复方面均优于高斯流匹配及其他重尾基线，同时保留了流匹配的低 NFE 采样优势。

Conclusion: 随机时钟公式提供了实用的尾部控制接口：仅改变时钟分布或尾部参数，同一架构即可校准不同分布族的生成尾部"厚重"程度。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed data arise in many domains where rare events carry disproportionate importance, such as imbalanced image datasets, financial returns, and weather extremes. Standard diffusion and flow-matching models typically begin from Gaussian noise or Gaussian source distributions, which yield tractable training targets but provide a poor inductive match for heavy-tailed data. We propose Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching via Random Clocks (HTFM), a framework that portrays heavy-tailed sources as mixtures of clock-conditioned Gaussian sources. Conditioning on a given clock path, the source distribution and flow are Gaussian; marginalizing over the clock gives a Gaussian scale mixture covering Gaussian, $α$-stable, and Student-t families. To make the clock-conditioned vector field practical, we encode the path-valued clock using truncated logsignature features, allowing the velocity field to adapt to the realized conditional space with negligible overhead. Empirically, on 2D imbalanced $α$-stable mixtures, CIFAR10-LT, and HRRR weather fields, HTFM improves mode coverage, sample quality, and tail-statistic recovery over Gaussian flow matching and competitive heavy-tailed baselines, while retaining the low-NFE sampling advantage of flow matching. Moreover, the random-clock formulation further provides a practical tail-control interface: by varying only the clock law or tail parameter, the same architecture can calibrate the ``heaviness'' of generated tails across different distribution families.

</details>


### [184] [CoDiffGRN: Rethinking Gene Regulatory Network Inference via the BEELINE-KGC Benchmark and Co-evolutionary Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13120)
*Jiaze Song,Runhao Zhao,Minghao Xu,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新将基因调控网络推断定义为归纳式、以排序为中心的图补全问题，提出新基准benchmark和共演化离散扩散框架method，在发现新调控关系上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRN推断方法与实际需求存在根本性错位：研究者通常需要少量高置信度调控交互用于实验验证（常涉及未见过的基因），但当前基准依赖直推式划分和全局分类指标，主流模型在归纳式场景下泛化能力差。

Method: 1) 将GRN推断重构为归纳式、排序为中心的图补全问题；2) 提出新基准benchmark，采用归纳式基因留存划分结合知识图谱补全指标评估top-ranked预测；3) 提出method——首个共演化离散扩散框架，联合建模生物学一致的离散化基因表达状态与调控交互；4) 引入TF-ALL子图采样策略(TASS)实现可扩展训练。

Result: 在benchmark上，method建立了新的SOTA性能，在新调控关系发现方面显著优于现有方法，消融实验验证了设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过问题重构、新基准设计和共演化离散扩散框架，method有效解决了GRN推断中归纳泛化和top-ranked预测的挑战，为实际生物发现提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Inferring gene regulatory networks (GRNs) from single-cell transcriptomic data is crucial for biological discovery, yet existing approaches suffer from a fundamental misalignment with real-world needs. Researchers typically seek a small set of high-confidence regulatory interactions for experimental validation, often involving previously unseen genes. However, current benchmarks rely on transductive splits with global classification metrics, while prevailing models struggle to generalize under inductive settings. To bridge this gap, we reformulate GRN inference as an inductive, ranking-centric graph completion problem and introduce \textbf{\benchmark}, a new benchmark that incorporates an inductive gene-holdout split together with knowledge graph completion metrics to better evaluate top-ranked predictions. Building on this, we propose \textbf{\method}, the first co-evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that jointly models biologically coherent discretized gene expression states and regulatory interactions for robust inductive generalization and improved top-ranked regulatory discovery. We further introduce TF-ALL Subgraph Sampling (TASS) for scalable training. Extensive experiments on {\benchmark} show that {\method} establishes new state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in novel regulatory discovery, and ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [185] [ShortOPD: Recovering Pruned LLMs with Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13124)
*Qingyu Zhang,Qianhao Yuan,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xiang Li,Ming Xu,Jiarui Li,Xiuyin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 结构化剪枝压缩大语言模型在生成任务上表现崩溃，本文发现其原因在于有用生成被降级而非擦除，且主要表现为后缀重复。作者提出Short-OPD，一种短到长的On-Policy Distillation调度策略，通过检测重复后缀并动态分配训练预算到有效长度，在数学、代码和开放式生成任务上将压缩模型得分提升约9倍，且仅用四分之一的训练时间即可匹配固定长序列的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝虽硬件友好，但压缩后的LLM在实际部署所需的自由形式生成任务上表现远差于多选题识别任务。作者观察到：贪婪解码下pass@1几乎消失，但pass@k通过重复采样可大幅恢复，且失败主要表现为后缀重复。这表明有用生成被"降级"而非"擦除"，可通过on-policy训练恢复，但现有on-policy方法将早期训练预算浪费在低信息量的重复后缀上。

Method: 提出Short-OPD（Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation）：一种基于压缩模型自身on-policy状态的训练调度策略。核心机制包括：(1) 检测教师模型确认的重复后缀；(2) 将未重复的前缀视为每个rollout的有效长度；(3) 将未来的rollout预算动态分配到策略当前能有效使用的长度上，实现从短到长的渐进式训练。

Result: 在数学、代码和开放式生成任务上，Short-OPD将压缩模型得分提升至未恢复时的约9倍，且是标准恢复方法（SFT w/o KD、KD、SeqKD）的1.6-4.4倍。该方法在使用固定8192-token rollout horizon的四分之一训练时间（8.5小时 vs 35.9小时）和减少71%的rollout token情况下，即可在2个点以内匹配其性能。

Conclusion: Short-OPD有效解决了结构化剪枝后LLM在生成任务上的崩溃问题，通过高效的短到长训练调度显著提升恢复效率。该方法有望推动结构化剪枝超越困惑度和多选题基准的边际收益，向实际部署级别的生成质量迈进。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a hardware-friendly way to compress LLMs, but it is mostly validated on multiple-choice recognition tasks, while the same compressed checkpoints can collapse on the free-form generation that deployment actually requires. Two observations trace this gap. First, greedy \textsc{pass}@$1$ nearly vanishes after compression, yet \textsc{pass}@$k$ recovers substantially under repeated sampling: useful generations are demoted, not erased. Second, the recoverable regime fails mainly through suffix repetition. Recovery should therefore train on the compressed model's own on-policy states with dense token-level supervision, which On-Policy Distillation (OPD) provides by reusing the pre-compression model as a frozen teacher. However, long on-policy rollouts spend early recovery budget on low-information repetitive suffixes, delaying loss descent. To mitigate this waste, we propose \textbf{\shortopd}, a short-to-long OPD schedule that detects teacher-confirmed repetitive suffixes, treats the surviving prefix as each rollout's effective length, and allocates future rollout budgets to the effective lengths the policy can currently use. Across math, code, and open-ended generation, \shortopd\ raises the compressed model's score to about $9\times$ its unrecovered value and $1.6$--$4.4\times$ standard recovery recipes (SFT w/o KD, KD, and SeqKD), and it matches a fixed $8192$-token rollout horizon within two points using a quarter of the training time ($8.5$ vs.\ $35.9$ hours) and $71\%$ fewer rollout tokens. We hope this recipe helps move structured pruning beyond marginal gains on perplexity and multiple-choice benchmarks, a step closer to deployment-ready generation quality.

</details>


### [186] [Linear Independent Component Analysis via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14081)
*Ashutosh Jha,Michel Besserve,Simon Buchholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OT-ICA算法，利用平方Wasserstein距离（W₂²）度量非高斯性以替代传统代理对比函数，通过梯度优化寻找使投影与标准高斯分布之间W₂²最大化的独立成分，在模拟和实际应用中均表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ICA算法因精确负熵优化不可行，只能依赖四阶累积量或参数化对数似然等代理对比函数，这些代理函数隐含分布假设，可能偏离真实数据分布。作者希望找到一种无需分布假设、直接度量非高斯性的方法来改进ICA。

Method: 提出以投影数据分布与标准高斯分布之间的平方Wasserstein距离（W₂²）作为非高斯性度量。理论上证明，当投影恰好恢复一个独立成分时，该Wasserstein距离达到最大。基于此，设计OT-ICA算法，通过基于梯度的优化方法寻找最大化该距离的投影方向。

Result: 在模拟数据上，OT-ICA在不同潜变量分布下均优于基于代理函数的传统方法。在EEG伪影去除和计量经济学价格发现两个实际应用中，OT-ICA在无分布假设条件下成功完成ICA任务。

Conclusion: Wasserstein距离为ICA提供了一种无需分布假设的非高斯性度量，OT-ICA算法在理论和实证上均有效，可作为经典ICA方法的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) recovers jointly independent source signals from their linear mixtures. To achieve this, classical ICA algorithms attempt to maximize non-Gaussianity, measured by negentropy, which is linked to independence by information theory. Because exact negentropy optimization is intractable, they rely on proxy contrast functions, such as fourth-order cumulants, and parametric log-likelihoods. We propose instead to measure non-Gaussianity using the squared Wasserstein distance $W_2^2$ to a standard Gaussian. We prove that the Wasserstein distance between a standard normal distribution and linear projections of the data is maximized when the projection recovers an independent component. Based on this observation, we propose the OT-ICA algorithm which finds this projection by gradient-based optimization. Empirical evaluation on simulated data shows that OT-ICA outperforms proxy-based methods for different distributions of the latent variables. Application to EEG artifact removal and econometric price discovery confirm OT-ICA can be used for applied ICA tasks without distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [187] [HEDGEHOG: Hierarchical Evaluation of Drug Generators Through Rigorous Filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13155)
*Daria A. Ryabchenko,Pavel Gurevich,Shamil Kadyrov,Daria Frolova,Kseniia Fedisheva,Sergei A. Nikolenko,Alexander Shapeev,Marina A. Pak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEDGEHOG 是一个六阶段分子生成器过滤基准，模拟工业界苗头化合物筛选流程。对23个模型生成的23万分子测试，仅0.65%能通过全部阶段，揭示了当前分子生成器在同时满足药物化学、合成可行性、分子对接和3D构象等多重约束方面的核心局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分子生成器的常用评估指标（如有效性、新颖性、唯一性等）与药物化学实际需求脱节，无法真实反映生成化合物是否具有药用合理性，容易导致模型评估出现假阳性、误导研究方向、浪费计算资源。需要一个更贴近工业界实际筛选流程的标准化评估框架。

Method: 提出 HEDGEHOG 六阶段过滤基准：(1) 预处理；(2) 理化描述符筛选；(3) 结构警报和图合理性检查；(4) 合成可行性评估；(5) 分子对接和结合亲和力估计；(6) 三维构象与相互作用检查。在三类模型（共23个分子生成器）上采用统一协议进行评估，总计分析约23万个生成分子。

Result: 在23万个生成分子中，仅约0.65%能通过全部六个阶段的过滤。大多数分子在孤立指标下看似可接受，但极少数能同时满足理化性质、药物化学规则、合成可行性、对接得分和3D结合构象等多重约束。

Conclusion: 当前分子生成器的核心局限在于：单指标表现良好并不等同于多维度综合可用。HEDGEHOG 揭示了生成模型在真实药物发现场景中的短板，为未来分子生成器的开发和评估提供了更严格、更贴近工业实践的标准化基准。

Abstract: Generative molecular models can support early drug discovery by proposing new candidate compounds de novo. In practice, useful candidates must balance target-relevant activity, synthetic accessibility, physicochemical properties, and other multiparameter design constraints. However, metrics commonly used to evaluate molecular generators only weakly reflect whether the generated compounds are medicinally plausible and suitable for downstream computation. This can produce false positives in model evaluation, incorrect assumptions, and inefficient use of computational resources. We introduce HEDGEHOG, a unified six-stage filtration benchmark that is inspired by industrial hit identification workflows: (i) preprocessing; (ii) physicochemical descriptor screening; (iii) structural alerts and graph-sanity checks; (iv) synthesis feasibility; (v) docking and binding affinity estimation; and (vi) three-dimensional pose and interaction checks. We evaluate 23 molecular generators across three model classes under a standardized protocol. Across 230,000 generated molecules, only 0.65% of initial molecules survive all stages. Our results expose a central limitation of current molecular generators: molecules that appear acceptable under isolated criteria rarely satisfy medicinal chemistry, synthesis, docking, and 3D pose filters simultaneously.

</details>


### [188] [SteinGate: Tail-Sensitive Safe Reinforcement Learning via Stein Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13175)
*Yassine Chemingui,Chenhua Fan,Honghao Wei,Janardhan Rao Doppa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SteinGate 提出一种基于核化 Stein 差异（Kernelized Stein Discrepancy）的分布级安全证书，通过检验策略 rollout 成本分布与安全参考分布的一致性来检测尾部风险，并据此动态切换学习策略，在保持竞争力的回报的同时显著降低训练过程中的约束违反频率和严重程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常仅约束期望累积成本，难以检测罕见但灾难性的尾部事件；而基于尾部拟合的方法不够稳健。因此需要一种不依赖参数化尾部拟合、能有效检测成本分布尾部异常的可靠安全证书。

Method: 提出 SteinGate 安全证书，核心包括：(1) 使用核化 Stein 差异（KSD）进行分布一致性检验，替代脆弱的尾部拟合；(2) 考虑因成本截断（clipped costs）产生的边界原子（boundary atoms），使证书具备边界感知能力；(3) 利用该证书动态调整学习机制——当 rollout 成本分布与安全参考分布一致时推进奖励优化策略更新，当成本尾部偏离时切换至恢复策略。

Result: 在连续控制基准实验上，SteinGate 相较于现有最先进基线方法，显著降低了训练期间约束违反的频率和严重程度，同时保持了有竞争力的回报水平。

Conclusion: SteinGate 通过分布级的一致性检验提供了一种更可靠的安全强化学习范式，有效克服了期望约束和尾部拟合方法的不足，在安全性和性能之间实现了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning typically enforces safety by bounding expected cumulative costs, a criterion that often fails to detect rare but catastrophic tail events. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces SteinGate, a boundary-aware distributional safety certificate that replaces fragile tail fitting with a robust consistency check using Kernelized Stein Discrepancy while accounting for boundary atoms induced by clipped costs. SteinGate evaluates whether observed policy rollout costs remain consistent with a safe reference distribution, providing a non-parametric safety certificate. This certificate is used to dynamically adapt the learning regime: favoring reward-improving policy updates when rollouts remain consistent with the safe reference and switching to recovery behavior when the cost tail deviates. Experiments on continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate that SteinGate significantly reduces both the frequency and severity of constraint violations during training while maintaining competitive returns relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [189] [Concurrent Image Understanding and Generation: Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13188)
*Minh-Quan Le,Armand Comas,Alexandros Lattas,Stylianos Moschoglou,Pedro Vélez,Amit Raj,Aaron Germuth,Thabo Beeler,Dimitris Samaras,Di Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自校正耦合马尔可夫跳跃过程（SC-CMJP）框架及CO₂Jump采样器，实现文本与图像在扩散生成过程中的实时跨模态耦合与自校正，在联合多模态生成任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知中理解与生成并不分离，教师在白板上边说边画，两种模态相互塑造。然而现有Masked Diffusion Models的采样器要么交错解码文本和图像，要么在并行分支中独立更新，仅共享上一步历史而无法在同一步内获取对方模态的最新决策；加之MDMs缺乏重掩码机制，跨模态矛盾无法被检测和修复。这促使作者设计一个能实现实时跨模态耦合与纠错的框架。

Method: 提出SC-CMJP框架：一个模态的转移率是另一模态置信度分数的泛函，由跨模态注意力加权。引入重掩码跳跃机制，当跨模态证据与当前决策矛盾时立即撤回。基于此框架设计CO₂Jump——一种免训练的单次采样器，用于联合多模态生成。同时构建并发布三个大规模联合多模态生成数据集：JEdit-1M、JMaze-200K、JNono-200K，配以分布内和分布外基准。

Result: CO₂Jump在图像理解与编辑以及视觉推理（迷宫和非ograms求解）上均取得最佳联合性能。采样器性能随去噪步数单调递增，证明跨模态耦合的收益沿轨迹复合累积。

Conclusion: SC-CMJP框架与CO₂Jump采样器成功将人类认知中的耦合循环引入人工系统，通过实时跨模态交互与自校正机制，显著提升了联合多模态生成的质量与一致性。

Abstract: Human cognition does not separate understanding and generation. A teacher at a whiteboard speaks and draws $\textit{together}$, each modality reshapes the other. In this paper, we bring this coupled loop to artificial systems. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) are ideally suited to this task, yet existing samplers either decode text and image interleavedly or independently update them in parallel branches that share only previous-step history, but not the other modality's latest decisions $\textit{within}$ the same step; combined with MDMs' inability to remask, cross-modal contradictions are neither detected nor repaired. We introduce $\textbf{Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes (SC-CMJP)}$, a framework in which one modality's transition rates are functionals of the other modality's confidence score, as weighted by cross-modal attention. Furthermore, a remasking jump retracts commitments the moment cross-modal evidence turns against them. In conjunction with SC-CMJP, we introduce $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ (Self-$\underline{\text{CO}}$rrecting $\underline{\text{CO}}$upled $\underline{\text{Jump}}$), a novel training-free single-pass sampler for joint multimodal geneneration. For training and evaluation purposes, we have created and will release three large-scale joint multimodal generation corpora: $\text{JEdit-1M}$, $\text{JMaze-200K}$, $\text{JNono-200K}$, with matching in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks. $\texttt{CO}_\texttt{2}\texttt{Jump}$ achieves best joint performance for image understanding and editing as well as visual reasoning (maze and nonogram solving). The performance of the sampler scales monotonically with the number of denoising steps, evidence that the benefits of cross-modal coupling $\textit{compound}$ across the trajectory. Project page: https://coupled-jump.github.io

</details>


### [190] [EMAGN: Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network via Learned Clustering for Scalable Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13241)
*Mingxing Xu,Rakesh Chowdary Machineni,Ke Liu,Xi Cheng,Chengqi Lu,Xin Hu,Lyuhao Chen,Xiangyu Li,Junwei You,Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效多注意力图网络(EMAGN),通过自适应聚类将空间注意力的复杂度从O(N²d)降至O(NMd),在保持接近全注意力精度的同时,显著降低了训练/推理时间和GPU内存消耗,并突破了全注意力机制在大规模图上的内存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通预测中自注意力机制虽能有效建模动态长程依赖,但其二次方计算与内存复杂度导致可扩展性受限,尤其在大规模图和多头注意力场景下极易耗尽GPU内存。需要一种既能保持注意力灵活性又具备线性复杂度的替代方案。

Method: 提出EMAGN,灵感源自快速高维高斯滤波理论。核心思想是通过两个可学习的聚类矩阵C_k和C_v,将键(key)和值(value)向量自适应地分组为M个超类(super-clusters),从而将空间注意力计算复杂度从O(N²d)线性化至O(NMd)。该方法在GMAN骨干网络中实现,并与Linformer、Performer等线性注意力方案进行对比。

Result: 在PEMS-BAY和METR-LA数据集上,EMAGN的MAE与全注意力GMAN相比仅差2.7-3.2%,但训练时间减少32%、推理时间减少38%、GPU内存减少58%。在K=16个注意力头时,全注意力GMAN在11 GB GPU上直接内存溢出,而EMAGN仍可正常运行。EMAGN在精度与效率上均优于同骨干下的Linformer和Performer。

Conclusion: EMAGN通过在空间注意力中引入交通网络感知的自适应聚类,成功实现了精度与效率的良好平衡,并大幅拓展了可行模型配置的范畴,为大规模交通预测提供了一种实用的高效注意力解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is highly challenging due to complex and nonlinear spatial and temporal dependencies. Self-attention mechanisms have been widely adopted to model dynamic and long-range dependencies, achieving state-of-the-art performance, but suffer from limited scalability due to quadratic computational and memory complexity. To address this, we propose an Efficient Multi-Attention Graph Network (EMAGN) that linearises the spatial attention mechanism itself, inspired by the theory of fast high-dimensional Gaussian filtering. Two learned clustering matrices C_k and C_v adaptively group key and value vectors into M super-clusters, reducing complexity from O(N^2 d) to O(NMd) without sacrificing the flexibility of attention for dynamic dependency modelling. Experimental results on PEMS-BAY and METR-LA show that EMAGN achieves accuracy within 2.7-3.2% MAE of full-attention GMAN while reducing training time by 32%, inference time by 38%, and GPU memory by 58%. Critically, at K=16 attention heads, full-attention GMAN runs out of memory on a standard 11 GB GPU entirely while EMAGN continues to operate, demonstrating a categorical expansion of feasible model configurations. EMAGN also surpasses Linformer and Performer in both accuracy and efficiency within the same backbone, owing to its traffic-network-aware adaptive clustering.

</details>


### [191] [Reassessing Muon for Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13246)
*Ali Parviz,Gal Mishne,Alex Cloninger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器通过近似正交化重塑梯度更新，在大模型训练中表现优异，但本文在低秩矩阵分解这一简单受控任务上发现，Muon并未一致优于AdamW，其优势对超参数选择敏感，呼吁在受控问题上评估现代优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在大规模语言模型训练中表现突出，但其优势究竟来自更新规则本身，还是来自现代深度网络的规模、架构和数据等混杂因素，尚不明确。因此需要在剥离这些混杂因素的简单受控问题上进行隔离分析。

Method: 将Muon优化器应用于低秩矩阵分解这一具有明确谱结构的简单问题上，与经过精心调参的自适应基线优化器（如AdamW）进行受控对比实验，系统考察不同超参数设置下的性能表现。

Result: 在低秩矩阵分解任务上，Muon并未一致性地优于AdamW；此前文献报告的若干优势对超参数选择高度敏感，在特定超参数配置下这些优势会消失甚至反转。

Conclusion: 频谱感知的正交化并非在所有场景下都有益，其优势依赖于具体问题设置和超参数选择。评估现代优化器时，除端到端基准测试外，还应纳入受控问题上的对比实验，以更准确地归因优化器本身的效果。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a strong optimizer for large-scale deep learning, where it reshapes gradient updates through approximate orthogonalization and has been reported to outperform Adam and AdamW in large language model training. Its empirical success has motivated a growing body of theoretical work that interprets Muon as steepest descent under the spectral norm. Yet it remains unclear which of Muon's advantages stem from its update rule itself and which are artifacts of the scale, architecture, and data of modern deep networks. In this work, we isolate the optimizer from these confounding factors by studying Muon on a simple, well-understood, and spectrally structured problem: low-rank matrix factorization. Through a controlled comparison against carefully tuned adaptive baselines, we find that Muon does not consistently outperform AdamW in this setting and that several previously reported advantages are sensitive to hyperparameter choices. Our results provide a more nuanced picture of when spectrum-aware orthogonalization is beneficial and argue for evaluating modern optimizers on controlled problems in addition to end-to-end benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [Deconstructing Actor-Critic: A Large-scale Empirical Study of Design Components for Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13274)
*Haseeb Shah,Lingwei Zhu,Adam White,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过33,000+次实验，系统分析了Actor-Critic算法中关键设计组件（策略更新方式、动作分布、梯度估计器、更新频率）对算法可靠性和超参数敏感性的影响。结果显示，常见默认配置（如高斯动作分布+路径梯度估计器）是最不可靠的，而有界分布配合自适应更新调度在所有设置下均保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习正被应用于对可靠性要求极高、调参预算有限的真实世界控制系统（如聚变等离子体、自动驾驶、水处理等），但Actor-Critic算法涉及多个关键设计决策，这些决策如何影响算法的可靠性和鲁棒性缺乏系统的实证研究指导。

Method: 基于真实水处理厂的控制任务，对Actor-Critic算法的四个关键设计维度进行大规模消融实验：(1) 策略更新方式；(2) 动作分布表示（如高斯分布、有界分布等）；(3) 梯度估计器（路径梯度、得分函数等）；(4) 策略与值函数更新的相对频率。共运行超过33,000次实验，系统评估各组件对运行间变异性和超参数敏感性的影响。

Result: 常见默认配置——高斯动作分布配合路径梯度估计器（pathwise gradient estimator）——属于最不可靠的配置之一；而有界动作分布（bounded distributions）配合自适应更新调度（adaptive update schedules）在广泛的超参数设置范围内始终保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 为跨科学与工程领域的实践者提供了清晰的组件级决策指导：在将Actor-Critic方法应用于新的真实世界控制任务时，应优先选择有界动作分布和自适应更新调度，而非盲目依赖传统默认配置。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly being considered for controlling real-world systems, from fusion plasma and autonomous vehicles to drug discovery and drinking water treatment, where reliability is essential and tuning budgets are limited. Actor-critic algorithms share a set of design decisions, such as how the policy is updated, how it represents the distribution over actions, how its gradient is estimated, and how often it is updated relative to the value estimator. Using a control task derived from a real water treatment plant, we analyze over 33,000 experiments to determine how these components affect variability across runs and sensitivity to hyperparameters. Common defaults, such as Gaussian action distributions with pathwise gradient estimators, are among the least reliable configurations, whereas bounded distributions with adaptive update schedules remain robust across a wide range of settings. These findings offer empirical guidance to practitioners across scientific and engineering domains for understanding and making component-level decisions when adapting actor-critic methods to new real-world control settings.

</details>


### [193] [Tabular Foundation Models for Discrete Choice Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13314)
*Liu Liu,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将表格基础模型（TFMs）应用于离散选择建模，通过重新编码选择集依赖和个体异质性，在酸奶扫描面板数据上，最佳重构方法在留存对数似然上超越分层贝叶斯估计8%，命中率提升3.6%，且运行速度快16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散选择是营销与运营中核心的需求估计框架，但现有表格基础模型假设观测行间独立，无法处理离散选择的两个本质特征：（1）选择是集合值（choice-set dependence）的；（2）消费者偏好存在持久的个体异质性。直接将TFMs应用于该领域效果有限，因此需要弥合这一结构性鸿沟。

Method: 作者提出一种重构方法，在基于行的学习框架中同时编码选择集依赖和个体异质性。具体包括：（1）将选择集信息编码到输入表示中；（2）引入个体层面的异质性编码机制。在酸奶扫描面板数据上进行评估，对比不同重构策略的预测表现，并与分层贝叶斯估计基准进行比较。同时考察了在中数据区间（每位消费者10-40次购买）的表现，以及在总体选择数据上微调带来的增益。

Result: （1）个体层面异质性编码是预测准确性的主导驱动因素；（2）最佳重构方法在留存对数似然上超越分层贝叶斯8%，命中率提升3.6%，运行速度快16倍；（3）优势在中等数据区间（每位消费者10-40次购买）最大，因为参数化贝叶斯收缩对非典型消费者的估计扭曲最严重；（4）在总体选择数据上微调可为购买历史较浅的消费者提供额外增益。

Conclusion: 该研究为将基础模型应用于消费者选择问题建立了原则性方法，证明通过适当的结构化重构，TFMs可以在离散选择领域超越传统分层贝叶斯方法，尤其在大规模需求估计中具有显著的计算效率优势。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) generate predictions on structured data via in-context learning, without task-specific estimation. We ask whether TFMs can be effectively applied to discrete choice, a central demand estimation framework in marketing and operations, and find that directly applying TFMs yields limited performance. The gap is structural: TFMs assume row-independent observations, whereas discrete choice is inherently set-valued and subject to persistent consumer preference heterogeneity. We propose a reformulation that encodes both choice-set dependence and individual heterogeneity within a row-based learning framework. Evaluated on a yogurt scanner panel, individual-level heterogeneity encoding is the dominant driver of predictive accuracy. The best reformulation outperforms hierarchical Bayesian estimation by 8\% in holdout log-likelihood and 3.6\% in hit rate, running 16 times faster, a practical advantage for large-scale demand estimation. The advantage is largest in the medium-data regime (10--40 purchase occasions per consumer), where parametric Bayesian shrinkage most distorts estimates for atypical consumers. Fine-tuning on population choice data provides additional gains for consumers with shallow purchase histories, where in-context learning has limited individual-specific signal to condition on. These results establish a principled approach for applying foundation models to consumer choice problems more broadly.

</details>


### [194] [Accuracy-Preserving Stability Regularization for Large-Scale Retail Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13331)
*Jize Li,Jiani He,Dishu Yang,Dingyan Shang,Jingjing Liu,Shiqi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在训练阶段对连续预测值之间的波动施加惩罚，以提升零售需求预测路径的横向稳定性。在M5数据集上，稳定性感知的混合模型相比XGBoost在预测稳定性得分上提升约7%，而RMSE变化控制在0.72%以内，优于事后指数平滑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售需求预测被复用于补货、产能、劳动力和运输等多个规划周期。传统的点误差目标函数并不约束相邻预测值之间的剧烈波动，而事后平滑仅在模型拟合后起作用，且往往以较大的点精度损失为代价。因此，作者希望探索在训练阶段直接引入对序列内连续波动的惩罚，能否在不显著损害点精度的前提下提升预测路径的横向稳定性。

Method: 提出一种时间结构化流水线（temporal-structured pipeline），融合近期需求嵌入（recent-demand embeddings）与日历、价格、层级、商品和门店等特征。在该框架中引入训练时稳定性正则化惩罚项，直接约束连续预测值之间的波动。将该稳定性感知混合模型与XGBoost基线及事后指数平滑方法进行对比，采用M5数据集中1000、3000和4000序列三个规模进行实验，使用三种随机种子评估RMSE和预测稳定性得分（Forecast Stability Score）。

Result: 在M5数据集的三个规模上，稳定性感知混合模型相比XGBoost分别提升预测稳定性得分6.91%、6.66%和7.68%，而RMSE变化在三种随机种子下均控制在0.72%以内。事后指数平滑虽然实现了更低的原始波动幅度，但付出了更高的RMSE代价；训练时正则化在保持点精度的同时，在归一化稳定性指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: 该研究将预测评估从单纯的点误差最小化拓展到了面向运营级零售预测的精度-稳定性权衡视角。在训练阶段引入稳定性正则化是一种比事后平滑更优的策略，能够在几乎不牺牲点精度的前提下显著改善预测路径的平滑性，对多周期联动的零售运营决策具有实际价值。

Abstract: Retail demand forecasts are reused across replenishment, capacity, labor, and transportation planning cycles. Point-error objectives do not constrain abrupt movement between adjacent forecasts, while post-hoc smoothing acts only after model fitting. We ask whether a training-time penalty on consecutive within-series movement can improve horizontal forecast-path stability without materially changing point accuracy. The penalty is evaluated in a temporal-structured pipeline combining recent-demand embeddings with calendar, price, hierarchy, item, and store features. On selected M5 demand series at 1000, 3000, and 4000-series scales, the stability-aware hybrid model improves Forecast Stability Score over XGBoost by 6.91%, 6.66%, and 7.68%, respectively, while RMSE changes remain within 0.72% across three random seeds. Post-hoc exponential smoothing attains lower raw movement but incurs a larger RMSE cost; training-time regularization preserves more point accuracy and performs favorably under normalized stability. These findings extend forecast evaluation from point-error minimization toward an accuracy-stability trade-off perspective for operational retail forecasting.

</details>


### [195] [Agora: Collective and Permissionless Internet-Scale Pretraining of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13332)
*Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Karol Pajak,James Snewin,Harry Xi,Rodney O'Donnell,Thalaiyasingam Ajanthan,Sameera Ramasinghe,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Shamane Siriwardhana,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agora 是一个去中心化的大模型训练系统，通过互联网连接异构消费级GPU，利用带宽高效的流水线并行和多方容错集合操作，实现了8.6B参数模型在330个贡献节点上持续40天的预训练，效率达到集中式H100基线的63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模语言模型训练被局限于数据中心，依赖同构加速器、高速互联和单一管理实体，导致前沿模型开发集中在少数机构。同时，大量消费级和专业级GPU（异构、可抢占、个人拥有、仅通过互联网连接）处于闲置状态。本研究的动机在于充分利用这部分被浪费的计算资源，实现开放、无需许可的前沿模型训练。

Method: 提出Agora系统，核心包括：(1) 带宽高效的流水线并行模型分片，适配互联网级链路的低带宽特性；(2) 多方容错集合操作，支持节点随时加入和退出；(3) Protocol Learning机制——每个参与者仅持有模型的一个阶段（stage），任何单一实体均不持有完整权重，实现集体训练、集体所有的模型。系统整合了通信高效并行、异步优化和容错系统设计三大技术方向。

Result: 成功完成Pluralis-8B实验：在500B tokens的FineWeb-Edu数据上预训练8.6B参数模型，历时40天，330个贡献节点参与（主要为互联网连接的消费级GPU），节点可随时加入/退出。训练吞吐量约170k tokens/s，每TFLOP池化算力产出4.2 tokens，达到集中式H100基线效率的63%，模型收敛至与集中式参考运行仅有较小差距。

Conclusion: Agora证明了在去中心化、异构、不可靠的互联网计算资源上进行前沿规模LLM预训练的可行性。Protocol Learning范式使得集体训练、集体所有的开放模型成为可能，为开源前沿模型训练的经济可持续性开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Training large language models at the multi-billion to trillion parameter scale is confined to datacenters, where data-parallel (DP) and model-parallel (MP) techniques presume homogeneous accelerators, high-speed interconnects, and a single orchestrating entity. Frontier model development is thereby concentrated among the few groups able to assemble such clusters. Meanwhile, an enormous pool of compute remains unusable for training: consumer and professional GPUs that are heterogeneous, preemptible, individually owned, and connected only by the internet. We present Agora, a system that makes efficient use of this compute. Agora combines bandwidth-efficient pipeline-parallel model sharding over internet-grade links with multi-party, fault-tolerant collective operations. Each participant holds only one stage of the model, and no single party ever possesses the full weights. We term this setup Protocol Learning: it enables collectively trained, collectively owned models, opening a path to open-source frontier training with economic sustainability. This report presents the outcome of a research effort spanning communication-efficient parallelism, asynchronous optimization, and fault-tolerant systems design. It culminates in the first demonstration of its kind: Pluralis-8B, an open, permissionless pretraining run of an 8.6B-parameter model on 500B tokens of FineWeb-Edu. The model was trained over 40 days by 330 contributor nodes, predominantly consumer GPUs on internet connections, joining and leaving throughout. The run sustained ~170k tokens/s and 4.2 tokens per TFLOP of pooled compute, 63% of the efficiency of a centralized H100 baseline, and converged to within a small margin of a centralized reference run.

</details>


### [196] [Weight Feedback Computes the Jacobian Transpose Locally in Modern Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13380)
*Junlong Shen,Xingyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 WF-Act-PC，通过将 Jacobian 转置分解为三个局部可计算项，消除了预测编码中最后一个非局部操作——autograd 的反向传播，在 CIFAR 和 Tiny-ImageNet 上取得与调优后反向传播相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）虽然通过局部权重更新提供了生物可解释的反向传播替代方案，但层间误差传递仍依赖 autograd 的 Jacobian 转置（J^T）乘积，这是 PC 中最后一个非局部操作。作者旨在消除这一依赖性，实现完全局部的学习规则。

Method: 针对形式为 f(x)=Act(Norm(L(x))) 且归一化统计量被冻结的层，作者证明精确的 J^T 可分解为三个局部项：J^T v = L^T(s ⊙ σ'(z) ⊙ v)，其中 s=γ/σ_run 为归一化增益。该方法依赖三个假设：权重对称（L^T 镜像前向算子）、软谱范数控制（非突触局部）、以及 MaxPool 的最近邻近似。将此恒等式代入 PC 即得 WF-Act-PC，完全移除 autograd 反向传播。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100（50 epoch，5 种子）上，WF-Act-PC 是唯一精度随深度提升的 PC 方法，比最强经典 PC 基线 iPC 高出 2.7-22.3 个百分点。在更深架构上（VGG-9: 93.57% vs 92.43%; ResNet-18: 92.76% vs 91.54%）和 Tiny-ImageNet 上匹配或超越调优后的 BP 基线，但在更深 CIFAR-100 VGG 单元上仍落后。

Conclusion: J^T 的非局部性是 PC 中可避免的，通过恢复归一化增益和激活导数两项校正，WF-Act-PC 在保持局部性的同时实现了与 BP 相当的深层网络训练效果，向完全局部、生物可行的深度学习迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) offers a biologically motivated alternative to backpropagation via local weight updates, yet routing error between layers still relies on an autograd Jacobian-transpose ($J^\top$) product - the last non-local operation in PC. We show that this dependency is largely avoidable. For any layer $f(x)=\mathrm{Act}(\mathrm{Norm}(L(x)))$ with frozen normalization statistics, the exact $J^\top$ factors into three locally available terms, $J^\top v = L^\top(s \odot σ'(z) \odot v)$, where $σ'$ is the activation derivative, $z$ is the pre-activation, and $s=γ/σ_{\mathrm{run}}$ is the normalization gain. Prior weight-feedback methods omitted both corrections; restoring them closes the transport gap for this layer class. Locality here holds up to three assumptions, which we state upfront: weight symmetry ($L^\top$ mirrors the forward operator, as assumed by all PC), a soft spectral-norm control that is not synapse-local, and a nearest-neighbour approximation for MaxPool. Substituting the identity into PC yields WF-Act-PC, which removes the autograd backward pass from error transport. On CIFAR-10/100 (50 epochs, 5 seeds), WF-Act-PC is the only PC method whose accuracy improves with depth, surpassing iPC - the strongest classical PC baseline - by 2.7-22.3 pp on CIFAR-10. With both methods tuned per architecture, it matches or exceeds a comparably-tuned backpropagation baseline on the deeper CIFAR-10 architectures (VGG-9: 93.57% vs. 92.43%; ResNet-18: 92.76% vs. 91.54%) and on the harder Tiny-ImageNet benchmark, while trailing tuned BP on the deeper CIFAR-100 VGG cells. Our WF-Act-PC implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/jlshen025/pcax

</details>


### [197] [Where Should RL Post-Training Compute Go? Model Size, Search, Learning, and Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13389)
*Patrick Wilhelm,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习后训练中的固定预算决策问题，提出FLOP核算框架将计算分解为rollout/search、策略更新/学习和奖励反馈三部分，发现模型规模、训练分配与奖励系统之间存在条件分配前沿，并引入RACE诊断协议来在昂贵验证前识别分配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练普遍受限于单一总FLOP预算，但实践中缺乏系统性的指导：在相同预算下，究竟该用更大的策略模型、更长时间训练小模型、生成更多rollout搜索，还是将计算花在更强的奖励反馈上。现有工作通常只报告总预算而未细分计算分配，导致不同方法间难以公平比较。

Method: 提出面向GRPO后训练的FLOP核算框架，将计算分解为三部分：rollout/搜索、策略更新/学习、奖励或反馈模型评估。在LoRA适配的Qwen2.5策略上，系统性地变动模型大小、训练步数、rollout数量和奖励系统（基于规则的奖励 vs. PRM式反馈），绘制不同条件下的计算分配前沿。同时提出RACE诊断协议——一种轻量级网格扫描方案，用于在昂贵的大规模验证前识别有前景的分配区间。

Result: 发现条件分配前沿：最优分配随模型大小、计算预算、奖励系统和评估目标而变化。同FLOP下的模型规模比较表明，模型选择与训练分配相互耦合——大模型每token计算量更大，在相同预算下能购买的更新步数或rollout更少。奖励系统也改变计算账目：基于规则的奖励将几乎全部非更新计算用于策略rollout，而PRM式反馈则需将显著部分预算分配给奖励模型推理。

Conclusion: RL后训练论文应报告总FLOPs及其在模型规模、搜索、学习和反馈之间的细分，而非仅给出单一总预算。RACE协议可作为诊断工具，在昂贵验证前识别分配机制，但不能保证留出集上的提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training is increasingly used to adapt foundation models for reasoning, planning, and feedback-driven robot-learning pipelines, but constrained post-training resources are often summarized by a single total FLOP budget. We study the fixed-budget decision problem behind this practice: under the same post-training budget, should one use a larger policy, train a smaller policy longer, generate more rollout search, or spend compute on stronger reward feedback? We introduce a FLOP-accounting framework for GRPO post-training that decomposes compute into rollout/search, policy-update/learning, and reward- or feedback-model evaluation. Across LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5 policies, we find conditional allocation frontiers: the best observed allocation changes with model size, compute budget, reward system, and evaluation target. Same-FLOP model-size comparisons show that model choice and training allocation are coupled because larger policies consume more per-token compute and therefore buy fewer updates or rollouts under the same budget. Reward systems also change the accounting: rule-based rewards spend nearly all non-update compute on policy rollouts, while PRM-style feedback allocates a visible part of the budget to reward-model inference. We present RACE as a diagnostic pilot-grid protocol, not a guarantee of held-out improvement, for identifying allocation regimes before expensive validation runs; our results suggest that RL post-training papers should report total FLOPs together with how compute is divided among model size, search, learning, and feedback.

</details>


### [198] [Self-Improving is Often Sudden: Enlightenment-style Finetuning for Large-Scale Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13395)
*Jing-Xiao Liao,Tianwei Zhang,Yu-Hao Jiang,Feifei Zhang,Hang-Cheng Dong,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Enlightenment，一种无需训练的后调优范式，通过修改关键模块/层的捷径连接（而非注意力权重）来解锁预训练大模型的潜在能力。针对LLM提出注意力头混合捷径，针对VLM提出残差连接标量调制，在多种基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类大脑"顿悟"（aha moment）现象启发，作者假设大模型同样存在类似的"开悟"现象——即模型具有潜在的突然能力跃升空间。现有免训练方法多集中于操纵注意力权重，而作者探索通过修改模型内部的捷径连接来实现这一潜力释放，且无需权重更新，成本极低。

Method: 提出Enlightenment，一种训练无关的后调优范式：1）对LLM，引入注意力头混合捷径，将首个注意力头的输出连接到所有其他目标头，并通过自适应缩放因子初始化策略进行调制，以此重校准注意力权重；2）对VLM，在解码器层的残差连接上施加轻量级标量调制因子，调控信息流。两者均不涉及权重更新。

Result: 在多种基准测试和不同模型上的广泛实验表明，Enlightenment能高效释放预训练网络的潜在能力，带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: Enlightenment作为一种免训练的后调优方法，通过巧妙的捷径连接修改策略，以极低成本实现了大模型能力的显著跃升，验证了大模型存在"开悟"现象的假设，为模型能力的高效挖掘提供了新思路。

Abstract: The pursuit of autonomously self-improving models has attracted growing interest in the era of large-scale foundation models. Drawing inspiration from the concept of "enlightenment" or "aha moment" in human brain, we hypothesize that large models exhibit an analogous enlightenment phenomenon-a latent capacity for sudden capability boost. Then, we propose Enlightenment, a novel training-free post-tuning paradigm for large-scale models. Our approach modifies shortcuts for key modules/layers without weight updates, while existing training-free ones predominantly manipulate attention weights. We introduce two architecture-specific instantiations: i) For large language models, we propose attention head-mixing shortcuts that recalibrate attention weights by linking the initial attention head's output to all other target heads, modulated by an adaptive scaling factor initialization strategy. ii) For vision-language models, we apply a lightweight scalar-modulated factor to residual connections in the decoder layers, regulating information flow. Extensive experiments show that Enlightenment efficiently unlocks the latent potential of pre-trained networks, yielding remarkable performance improvements across diverse benchmarks and models.

</details>


### [199] [Is the Statistical Advantage Worth the Cost? An Empirical Comparison of KANs and MLPs for Structured Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13413)
*Matthew Steven P. Toledo,Justine Raphael H. Jacinto,Vivekjeet Singh Chambal,Rodolfo C. Camaclang,Jamlech Iram N. Gojo Cruz,Reginald Neil C. Recario*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对KAN与MLP在结构化表格分类任务上进行实证基准比较，发现KAN在二分类和多分类中统计显著优于MLP，但优势伴随更高的参数与计算复杂度，属于中等效应量（d=-0.46），需在精度与资源之间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN作为替代MLP的函数逼近架构日益受到关注，但缺乏在结构化表格数据上的系统性实证比较，需要明确二者在实际分类任务中的性能差异与适用场景。

Method: 在12个公开数据集（涵盖二分类、多分类、多标签和有序分类）上，采用标准化预处理、统一架构和固定超参数设置训练两种模型，以测试准确率和F1分数为指标，结合配对假设检验和效应量分析进行评估。

Result: KAN在二分类和多分类领域统计显著优于MLP，并在全部数据集上取得显著的聚合优势；但效应量为中等水平（d=-0.46），且KAN的参数量和计算复杂度远高于MLP基线。

Conclusion: KAN适合高精度应用场景，MLP仍是资源受限环境下的高效稳健选择；未来需扩展至更多数据模态以完善架构选择标准。

Abstract: This study presents an empirical benchmarking comparison between Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) and Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) on structured tabular classification tasks. Motivated by the growing interest in KANs as an alternative function-approximating architecture, we evaluate their out-of-the-box performance on twelve publicly available datasets spanning binary, multiclass, multilabel, and ordinal problems. Both models were trained under standardized preprocessing, architecture, and fixed hyperparameter settings, with performance assessed using test accuracy and F1-Score, paired hypothesis testing, and effect size analysis. Results show that KANs statistically outperform MLPs in binary and multiclass domains and achieve a significant aggregate advantage across all datasets. However, the observed medium effect size (d = -0.46) raises an important cost-benefit consideration: while KANs offer superior generalization through adaptive spline-based mappings, this advantage comes with substantially higher parameter and computational complexity relative to the MLP baseline. These findings suggest KANs are the preferred choice for high-precision applications, while MLPs remain a robust and efficient option for resource-constrained environments. Future work should extend this analysis to additional data modalities to further refine these architectural selection criteria.

</details>


### [200] [EXPLORE: Exploration with Guided Search for Analog Topology Generation using Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13416)
*Guanglei Zhou,Chen-Chia Chang,Yikang Shen,Jonathan Ku,Isaac Jacobson,Jingyu Pan,Yiran Chen,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXPLORE 框架将 MCTS 与 transformer 解码相结合，在测试时利用仿真器引导搜索，将 6 元件模拟电路拓扑生成成功率从 12%（单次生成）提升至 65%，MSE 降低超 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于序列到序列微调的一次性生成方法因搜索空间指数增长和训练数据有限，无法生成复杂模拟电路拓扑，亟需一种能在测试时扩展搜索能力的新方法。

Method: 提出 EXPLORE 框架，核心是将仿真器引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）与 transformer 解码相融合。利用语言模型先验跳过高置信度结构 token，将昂贵的仿真器预算集中分配到影响拓扑结构的关键决策上，实现测试时缩放。

Result: 在 6 元件、容差 0.01 的基准测试上：单次生成成功率为 12%，采样过滤基线为 33%，EXPLORE 达到 65%；在相同搜索预算下，MSE 相比采样过滤基线降低超 20%。

Conclusion: EXPLORE 是首个将结构化测试时搜索与大语言模型解码相结合用于模拟拓扑生成的框架，为扩展 LLM 驱动的设计自动化迈出了实用一步。

Abstract: Automating analog circuit topology design is essential to reduce the extensive manual effort required to meet increasingly diverse and customized application demands. Recent advances have applied sequence-to-sequence fine-tuning on pretrained language models to directly generate circuit topologies from user specifications in a single pass. However, these one-shot generation methods failed to generate complex circuits due to their exponentially growing search spaces and limited training datasets. In this paper, we present EXPLORE, a search-enhanced framework that integrates simulator-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with transformer-based decoding to enable test-time scaling for analog topology generation. By leveraging language-model priors and bypassing high-confidence structural tokens, EXPLORE allocates expensive simulator budget primarily toward topology-altering decisions during search. On a 6-component benchmark at a tight tolerance of 0.01, EXPLORE raises the success rate from 12% for one-shot generation and 33% for a sampling-and-filter baseline to 65%, and lowers MSE by over 20% relative to sampling-and-filter under the same search budget. These results establish EXPLORE as the first framework to integrate structured test-time search with LM decoding for analog topology generation, and a practical step toward scaling LLM-driven design automation.

</details>


### [201] [OrDA: Orthogonal Disentanglement of Access Habits Framework for Homepage Marketing Block Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13420)
*Lingxiao Zhang,Xiaobo Li,Tao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OrDA框架，通过正交解耦用户访问习惯与内容兴趣信号，消除推荐系统中营销位因访问习惯产生的伪阳性偏差，在线实验显示UCTR提升5.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主页营销位的点击受内容兴趣和访问习惯双重机制驱动。习惯性点击会造成“伪阳性”——位置优势掩盖平庸内容质量，导致推荐生态出现偏差。需要一种方法将纯兴趣信号从习惯噪声中分离出来。

Method: 提出OrDA（正交解耦访问习惯）框架：(1) 双塔结构配合门控分配层，自适应路由特征以最小化干扰；(2) 正交正则化约束潜在兴趣流形与习惯流形在几何上垂直，确保严格分离；(3) 推理阶段采用do-演算进行因果干预，仅依据纯化的兴趣评分进行排序。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的离线评估中，OrDA在预测精度上超越现有SOTA方法。在线A/B测试显示，芝麻主页营销位和租房推荐场景的用户点击率（UCTR）提升5.64%。

Conclusion: OrDA通过正交解耦与因果干预有效消除了访问习惯偏差，验证了将兴趣信号与习惯信号分离对于提升推荐系统公平性和准确性的重要性。

Abstract: Clicks on homepage marketing blocks are driven by a dual-mechanism of content interest and access habits. However, habitual clicks often create Pseudo-Positives in marketing slots, where position advantage masks mediocre content quality, leading to biased recommendation ecosystems. We propose a framework called Orthogonal Disentanglement of Access habits (OrDA) to purify interest signals. OrDA utilizes a dual-tower structure with a gated allocation layer to adaptively route features and minimize interference. To ensure rigorous separation, we employ orthogonal regularization to constrain the latent interest and habit manifolds to be geometrically perpendicular. OrDA performs causal intervention (do-calculus) during inference to rank items solely by purified interest scores. Empirical online evaluations on large-scale datasets demonstrate that OrDA effectively eliminates access-habit bias, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in predictive accuracy. Online AB test 5.64% shows user click-through rates (UCTR) improvement on the Zhima homepage marketing block, Zhima rent-floor recommendation.

</details>


### [202] [Temperature Scaling Is Not Enough: Calibration Gaps Under Human Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13423)
*Wisdom Dogah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了温度缩放校准方法在软标签（分布性标签）条件下的表现。发现基于硬标签校准的温度缩放在软标签场景下系统性表现不佳，存在"软标签校准差距"，且该差距在语言领域比视觉领域更大，并随模型规模单调增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度缩放作为主流后验校准方法，其理论前提是真实标签为独热且确定性的，但实践中标签常为软标签（如众包标注、分布性标签），反映人类标注者间的真实分歧。作者旨在检验当这一假设被违反时，温度缩放是否仍能保持校准性能，以及性能退化是否依赖模型规模。

Method: 使用CIFAR-10H（视觉）和ChaosNLI（语言）两个含人类标注软标签分布的数据集，在三种模型规模、两种模态下，分别用硬标签（多数投票）和软标签（分布性标签）训练温度缩放校准器，比较两者的Brier Score差距（软标签校准差距）。同时引入多类保序回归作为第二种后验校准基线进行验证。

Result: 全部九种配置均存在正向软标签校准差距（Brier Score差距0.002–0.134）。语言领域平均差距（0.079）远大于视觉领域（0.003）。视觉领域和ChaosNLI的SNLI子集上差距随模型规模单调增长。MNLI子集上出现规模排序反转，但归因于该子集准确率接近随机水平。多类保序回归得出相同定性结论。

Conclusion: 基于多数投票标签构建的校准协议在标签模糊具有结构性的场景中会系统性错误估计模型可靠性，对安全关键部署场景有直接影响。需在软标签条件下重新审视后验校准方法。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is the dominant post-hoc calibration method in modern deep learning. Its theoretical justification rests on an assumption that is rarely stated explicitly: that ground-truth labels are one-hot and deterministic. In practice, labels are frequently soft, crowd-sourced, or genuinely distributional, reflecting real disagreement among human annotators rather than annotation noise. We study whether temperature scaling retains its calibration properties when this assumption is violated, and whether any resulting degradation depends on model scale. Using CIFAR-10H and ChaosNLI, two publicly available datasets with human-annotated soft label distributions, we evaluate three model scales per modality under both hard one-hot and soft distributional label targets. Across all nine configurations we find a positive soft-label calibration gap: temperature scaling calibrated on hard labels consistently underperforms an oracle calibrated directly on soft labels, with Brier Score gaps ranging from 0.002 to 0.134. The gap grows monotonically with model scale in the vision domain and on the SNLI-derived split of ChaosNLI, and is substantially larger in the language domain (mean gap 0.079) than in vision (mean gap 0.003). A scale-ordering reversal on the MNLI-derived split remains after matched-domain training; we treat it as inconclusive for the scale hypothesis and attribute it primarily to near-chance accuracy on that split. As a second post-hoc baseline, multiclass isotonic regression yields the same qualitative conclusion: positive soft-label gaps in all nine configurations, and larger gaps in language than in vision. These findings suggest that calibration protocols built on majority-vote labels systematically misstate model reliability wherever label ambiguity is structural, with direct consequences for deployment in safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [203] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of LLMs via Attention Head Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13425)
*Tuomas Oikarinen,Zixiao Chen,Charlotte Siska,Tsui-Wei Weng,Chandan Singh,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力头重加权方法（AHR），通过仅为每个注意力头学习一个标量权重来适配大语言模型到新的文本分类任务，参数量仅为LoRA的1/200-1/1000，在少样本场景下性能更优，且学习到的权重具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全等领域，标注数据稀缺，大语言模型在少样本困难任务上仍表现不佳。现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）虽减少了参数量，但在极少样本下仍有改进空间。作者希望利用注意力头的功能特化进一步压缩可训练参数，提升少样本学习效果。

Method: 提出Attention Head Reweighting（AHR）方法：冻结预训练LLM的所有参数，仅在每个注意力头上学习一个标量权重，通过加权调节各注意力头的贡献来适配下游文本分类任务。该方法利用不同注意力头已具备的功能特化，仅需学习极少参数（约占总参数0.0001%）。

Result: 在多个开源文本分类数据集上的实验表明，AHR在少样本学习场景下性能优于LoRA等标准基线方法，而可训练参数量仅为后者的1/200至1/1000。此外，学习到的注意力头权重具有良好可解释性，可用于分析LLM中负责上下文学习能力的注意力头及其机制。
